                            emitExecutionNotice(
                                type = StrategyNotificationType.AUTOMATION_ACTION,
                                title = "Bet Placed: ${CurrencyManager.formatAmount(proposedStake)}",
                                message = "Confirmed on $activeGameName ($strategyLabel, Round $activeRoundId).",
                                occurrenceKey = "bet_ok_${actionId}",
                                priority = NotificationPriorityLevel.STANDARD,
                                cooldownMs = 0L
                            )
                            isActionInProgress.set(false)
                            onFinished?.invoke(true, "Bet confirmed: ${CurrencyManager.formatAmount(proposedStake)}")
                        }

                        // Independently check post-click acceptance before marking successful
                        val postClickScan = targetUiInteractor.refreshLiveTargets(null)
                        val liveButtonAtClick = postClickScan?.siteButtonState ?: gameDetection.gameState.value.siteButtonState
                        val acceptedImmediately = (postClickScan?.isCancelBtnDetected == true) ||
                            liveButtonAtClick == SiteButtonState.BET_QUEUED ||
                            liveButtonAtClick == SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE

                        if (acceptedImmediately || (targetUiInteractor !is AccessibilityTargetUiInteractor && postClickScan == null)) {
                            confirmBetSuccess()
                        } else {
                            val confirmJob = scope.launch {
                                try {
                                    var elapsed = 0L
                                    var confirmed = false
                                    while (elapsed < 1800L) {
                                        delay(100L)
                                        elapsed += 100L
                                        val rescan = targetUiInteractor.refreshLiveTargets(null)
                                        val btnState = rescan?.siteButtonState ?: gameDetection.gameState.value.siteButtonState
                                        if (rescan?.isCancelBtnDetected == true ||
                                            btnState == SiteButtonState.BET_QUEUED ||
                                            btnState == SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE
                                        ) {
                                            confirmed = true
                                            break
                                        }
                                    }
                                    if (confirmed) {
                                        confirmBetSuccess()
                                    } else {
                                        finishFailed(
                                            "Bet acceptance not confirmed by game (button stayed $liveButtonAtClick).",
                                            targetCoords = verifiedCoords,
                                            gestureDispatched = true,
                                            operation = "PLACE_BET",
                                            episodeTag = "bet_unconfirmed"
                                        )
                                    }
                                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                                    finishFailed(
                                        "Bet verification interrupted: ${t.message}",
                                        targetCoords = verifiedCoords,
                                        gestureDispatched = true,
                                        episodeTag = "bet_verify_err"
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            confirmJob.invokeOnCompletion {
                                if (confirmJob.isCancelled) {
                                    isActionInProgress.set(false)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // STEP 4: Configure & Verify Game-Side Auto Cash Out when enabled
                if (!config.gameAutoCashOutEnabled) {
                    proceedSteps5To7()
                } else {
                    val requestedTarget = config.gameAutoCashOutTarget.coerceAtLeast(1.01)
                    if (isWindowBudgetExceeded()) {
                        finishFailed("Betting window budget exceeded before Auto Cash Out.", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "window_timeout")
                        return@applyStake
                    }
                    publishStatusTicker("Step 4: Setting Game Auto Cash Out (${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", requestedTarget)})...")
                    val autoStartWallMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
                    targetUiInteractor.configureAutoCashOut(true, requestedTarget) { autoOk, autoErr ->
                        val autoElapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - autoStartWallMs
                        if (autoElapsed > autoCashOutTimeoutMs || isWindowBudgetExceeded()) {
                            finishFailed("Game Auto Cash Out timed out (${autoElapsed}ms > ${autoCashOutTimeoutMs}ms).", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "auto_timeout")
                            return@configureAutoCashOut
                        }
                        if (!autoOk) {
                            finishFailed(autoErr ?: "Game Auto Cash Out configuration failed.", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "auto_failed")
                            return@configureAutoCashOut
                        }
                        val step4Targets = targetUiInteractor.refreshLiveTargets(latestUiTargets)
                        if (step4Targets != null) latestUiTargets = step4Targets
                        if (step4Targets?.isAutoCashOutToggleChecked == false) {
                            finishFailed("Game Auto Cash Out toggle did not remain ON.", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "auto_toggle_off")
                            return@configureAutoCashOut
                        }
                        val verifiedTarget = step4Targets?.detectedAutoCashOutValue
                            ?: step4Targets?.detectedAutoCashOutText?.let { ScreenOcrAnalyzer.parseNumericAutoTargetToken(it) }
                        if (verifiedTarget != null && kotlin.math.abs(verifiedTarget - requestedTarget) > 0.01) {
                            finishFailed("Game Auto Cash Out target mismatch: expected ${requestedTarget}x, reads ${verifiedTarget}x.", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "auto_target_mismatch")
                            return@configureAutoCashOut
                        }
                        if (targetUiInteractor is AccessibilityTargetUiInteractor &&
                            (step4Targets?.isAutoCashOutToggleChecked != true || verifiedTarget == null)
                        ) {
                            finishFailed("Could not verify Game Auto Cash Out toggle and target (${requestedTarget}x).", operation = "AUTO_CASHOUT", episodeTag = "auto_unverified")
                            return@configureAutoCashOut
                        }
                        proceedSteps5To7()
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            finishFailed("Automation execution error: ${t.message}", episodeTag = "exec_exception")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Triggered when a round reaches CRASHED (Flew Away).
     * Records MultiplierRecord + exact crash timestamp, settles any active round bet,
     * and evaluates strategy rules for the next round.
     */
    fun handleRoundCompleted(
        multiplier: Double,
        config: StrategyConfig,
        limits: RiskLimits,
        platformName: String = gameDetection.gameState.value.platformName,
        currentTimeMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        observationOnly: Boolean = false
    ) {
        sessionObservedCrashes += 1
        publishSessionStats()

        if (observationOnly) {
            val trigger = strategyEngine.processCompletedRound(multiplier, config, currentTimeMs)
            val stratState = strategyEngine.getState()
            val triggerName = formatTriggerLabel(trigger, stratState)
            gameDetection.updateStrategyTelemetry(stratState, triggerName)
            return
        }

        val gs = gameDetection.gameState.value
        val activeGameId = gs.gameId.ifBlank { "unknown" }
        val activeGameName = gs.displayedGameName.ifBlank { "Unknown" }
        val resolvedCat = gs.latestCrashColorCategory.takeIf { gs.latestCrashTimestampMs == currentTimeMs }
            ?: ColorClassifier.fallbackCategoryForMultiplier(multiplier, config.lowThreshold, config.bigThreshold)
        val resolvedHex = gs.latestCrashColorHex.takeIf { gs.latestCrashTimestampMs == currentTimeMs && it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: resolvedCat.defaultHex

        val seq = liveCrashSeqCounter.incrementAndGet().toInt()
        val dedup = MultiplierRecord.buildFallbackDedupKey(
            gameId = activeGameId,
            platform = platformName,
            roundId = null,
            gameTimestampMs = null,
            observationTimestampMs = currentTimeMs,
            crashTimestamp = currentTimeMs,
            multiplier = multiplier,
            sequenceIndex = seq
        )
        scope.launch {
            repository.addMultiplierRecord(
                MultiplierRecord(
                    sessionId = gs.activeSessionId,
                    gameId = activeGameId,
                    multiplier = multiplier,
                    gameTimestampMs = null,
                    observationTimestampMs = currentTimeMs,
                    crashTimestamp = currentTimeMs,
                    platform = platformName,
                    timestampSource = TimestampSource.LOCAL_OBSERVATION,
                    collectionSource = CollectionSource.LIVE_CRASH,
                    collectionStatus = gameDetection.historyMetadata.value.status,
                    dedupKey = dedup,
                    sequenceIndex = seq
                )
            )
            repository.insertCrashRecord(
                CrashRecord(
                    sessionId = gs.activeSessionId,
                    gameId = activeGameId,
                    gameName = activeGameName,
                    multiplier = multiplier,
                    detectedColorHex = resolvedHex,
                    colorCategory = resolvedCat,
                    isPixelColorVerified = gs.isLatestCrashColorVerified,
                    crashTimestamp = currentTimeMs,
                    hourStartTimestamp = CalendarHourUtils.hourStartFor(currentTimeMs),
                    sourcePackage = gs.sourcePackage,
                    roundIdentifier = null,
                    confidence = gs.detectionConfidence.coerceAtLeast(0.8f),
                    manuallyCorrected = false,
                    collectionSource = CollectionSource.LIVE_CRASH,
                    dedupKey = dedup
                )
            )
        }

        // If a bet was placed in this round during BETTING_OPEN, settle it at CRASHED
        cashOutAttemptedThisRound = false
        sawCashOutAvailableInCurrentFlight = false
        inFlightWaitingNoticeEmitted = false
        currentRoundBetAttemptLockKey = null
        if (_automationState.value == AutomationState.RUNNING && currentRoundBetActive) {
            settleActiveRoundBet(
                stakeUsed = currentRoundStake,
                crashedMultiplier = multiplier,
                cashedOutInFlight = currentRoundCashedOut,
                triggerLabel = currentRoundTriggerLabel,
                config = config,
                limits = limits,
                platformName = platformName,
                currentTimeMs = currentTimeMs
            )
            setRoundBetActive(false)
            currentRoundCashedOut = false
            gameDetection.setBetPlaced(false)
        } else if (targetUiInteractor !is AccessibilityTargetUiInteractor &&
            _automationState.value == AutomationState.RUNNING &&
            strategyEngine.getState().isBettingSessionActive &&
            config.isAutoBetEnabled
        ) {
            // Direct round-completion fallback for non-UI synchronous unit tests only
            val stratBefore = strategyEngine.getState()
            val proposedStake = stratBefore.currentCalculatedStake
            val riskCheck = riskManager.validateBetExecution(
                proposedStake = proposedStake,
                currentSessionLoss = activeSessionLoss,
                currentRounds = activeSessionRounds,
                currentProgressionLevel = stratBefore.currentStakeLevel,
                currentBalance = resolveEffectiveBalance(),
                limits = limits,
                currentSessionProfit = activeSessionProfit
            )

            if (!riskCheck.isAllowed) {
                haltAutomationForLimit(riskCheck.violationReason ?: "Risk limit reached.")
            } else {
                val instructionBefore = strategyEngine.getPendingStakeInstruction()
                val effectiveMode = if (instructionBefore.isSessionActive &&
                    instructionBefore.stakeLevel == stratBefore.currentStakeLevel
                ) {
                    instructionBefore.progressionMode
                } else {
                    stratBefore.activeProgressionMode
                }

                targetUiInteractor.applyStake(
                    newStake = proposedStake,
                    progressionMode = effectiveMode,
                    fallbackX = null,
                    fallbackY = null
                ) {
                    targetUiInteractor.clickBet(null, null) { }
                }

                activeSessionRounds += 1

                StrategyEngine.triggerAction(
                    AutomationActionEvent(
                        actionType = ActionType.BET,
                        amountOrMultiplier = proposedStake,
                        reason = stratBefore.activeRuleLabel ?: "Auto Bet (Level ${stratBefore.currentStakeLevel})",
                        timestamp = currentTimeMs
                    )
                )

                settleActiveRoundBet(
                    stakeUsed = proposedStake,
                    crashedMultiplier = multiplier,
                    cashedOutInFlight = multiplier >= config.targetMultiplier,
                    triggerLabel = stratBefore.activeRuleLabel ?: "Auto Progression (Level ${stratBefore.currentStakeLevel})",
                    config = config,
                    limits = limits,
                    platformName = platformName,
                    currentTimeMs = currentTimeMs
                )
            }
        }

        // Now evaluate the crashed round against StrategyEngine rules for upcoming rounds
        val trigger = strategyEngine.processCompletedRound(multiplier, config, currentTimeMs)
        val stratState = strategyEngine.getState()
        val triggerName = formatTriggerLabel(trigger, stratState)
        gameDetection.updateStrategyTelemetry(stratState, triggerName)
        if (_automationState.value == AutomationState.RUNNING &&
            config.isAutoBetEnabled &&
            stratState.isBettingSessionActive &&
            trigger !is StrategyTrigger.None &&
            trigger !is StrategyTrigger.SessionTimedOut &&
            trigger !is StrategyTrigger.RangeWaitStarted
        ) {
            notifyStrategyWaitingToBet(triggerName ?: stratState.activeRuleLabel ?: "Active Strategy")
        }
    }

    private fun settleActiveRoundBet(
        stakeUsed: Double,
        crashedMultiplier: Double,
        cashedOutInFlight: Boolean,
        triggerLabel: String,
        config: StrategyConfig,
        limits: RiskLimits,
        platformName: String,
        currentTimeMs: Long
    ) {
        val isWin = cashedOutInFlight || crashedMultiplier >= config.targetMultiplier
        val payout = if (isWin) stakeUsed * config.targetMultiplier else 0.0
        val netProfit = payout - stakeUsed

        if (isWin) {
            sessionWins += 1
        } else {
            sessionLosses += 1
        }
        activeSessionProfit += netProfit
        activeSessionLoss = kotlin.math.max(0.0, -activeSessionProfit)
        onDailyNetOutcomeRecorded?.invoke(netProfit, currentTimeMs)

        val updatedState = strategyEngine.processBetOutcome(isWin, crashedMultiplier, config, currentTimeMs)
        if (!updatedState.isBettingSessionActive) {
            activeSequenceExposure = 0.0
            activeTargetMultiplierOverride = null
        }
        gameDetection.updateStrategyTelemetry(updatedState, formatTriggerLabel(updatedState.activeTrigger, updatedState))

        publishSessionStats()

        lastLifecycleNoticeStage = if (isWin) "ROUND_WON" else "ROUND_LOST"
        emitExecutionNotice(
            type = StrategyNotificationType.AUTOMATION_ACTION,
            title = if (isWin) {
                "Round Won (+${CurrencyManager.formatAmount(netProfit)})"
            } else {
                "Round Lost (-${CurrencyManager.formatAmount(stakeUsed)})"
            },
            message = "Crashed at ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", crashedMultiplier)} on $platformName ($triggerLabel).",
            occurrenceKey = "round_settled_${currentTimeMs}_${if (isWin) "w" else "l"}",
            priority = NotificationPriorityLevel.STANDARD,
            cooldownMs = 0L
        )

        // Immediately halt all automation gestures if Stop if Profit Reaches, Stop if Loss Reaches, or Daily Loss triggers
        enforceLimitsNow(limits, currentTimeMs)

        scope.launch {
            repository.addSessionLog(
                SessionLog(
                    timestamp = currentTimeMs,
                    platform = platformName,
                    roundNumber = activeSessionRounds,
                    detectedMultiplier = crashedMultiplier,
                    outcome = if (isWin) "WIN" else "LOSS",
                    stake = stakeUsed,
                    payout = payout,
                    netProfit = netProfit,
                    triggerReason = triggerLabel
                )
            )
        }
    }

    /**
     * Evaluates in-flight multipliers read via AccessibilityService.
     * When target multiplier is reached during an active bet and Cash Out is available,
     * triggers immediate calibrated CASH OUT hand and verifies site confirmation.
     */
    fun evaluateInFlightMultiplier(
        currentMultiplier: Double,
        config: StrategyConfig,
        siteButtonState: SiteButtonState = SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE,
        limits: RiskLimits = RiskLimits(),
        currentTimeMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
    ) {
        if (enforceLimitsNow(limits, currentTimeMs)) return
        val flightElapsedMs = if (inFlightStartedAtMs > 0L) (currentTimeMs - inFlightStartedAtMs).coerceAtLeast(0L) else Long.MAX_VALUE
        if (config.minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs > 0L && flightElapsedMs < config.minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs) {
            return
        }
        val cashOutAllowed = siteButtonState == SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE
        // Strictly obey independent isAutoCashOutEnabled flag (never couple to isAutoBetEnabled)
        val autoCashOutActive = config.automationMasterEnabled &&
            config.isAutoCashOutEnabled &&
            config.targetMultiplier >= 1.01
        // With cashOutUntrackedBets OFF only a bet THIS app placed (strategy bet or the manual hand) is auto cashed
        // out; a visible Cash Out button alone does not count as "has a bet".
        val hasTrackedOrManualBet = gameDetection.gameState.value.isBetPlaced ||
            currentRoundBetActive ||
            (config.cashOutUntrackedBets && config.isAutoCashOutEnabled && siteButtonState == SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE)
        val effectiveTarget = (activeTargetMultiplierOverride ?: config.targetMultiplier).coerceAtLeast(1.01)
        // When the game's own Auto Cash Out is armed, the app only acts as a backup slightly after the target so the
        // two never race each other for the same click.
        val backupMargin = if (config.gameAutoCashOutEnabled) 0.10 else 0.0
        val retryReady = cashOutAttemptCount < 3 &&
            (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastCashOutAttemptWallMs) >= 500L
        if (_automationState.value == AutomationState.RUNNING &&
            autoCashOutActive &&
            cashOutAllowed &&
            hasTrackedOrManualBet &&
            !currentRoundCashedOut &&
            !cashOutAttemptedThisRound &&
            retryReady &&
            currentMultiplier >= effectiveTarget + backupMargin
        ) {
            if (config.verifyButtonStateBeforeAction &&
                targetUiInteractor is AccessibilityTargetUiInteractor &&
                !targetUiInteractor.isButtonReady(expectBet = false, config = config)
            ) {
                return
            }

            // Refresh live screen targets right before Cash Out to prevent clicking wrong/disabled controls
            val uiTargets = targetUiInteractor.refreshLiveTargets(latestUiTargets)
            if (uiTargets != null) latestUiTargets = uiTargets
            if (uiTargets != null) {
                if (uiTargets.conflictReasons.isNotEmpty()) {
                    emitEpisodeFailureNotice("cashout_conflict", "Cash Out Blocked", uiTargets.conflictReasons.first())
                    return
                }
                uiTargets.detectCashOutCollisionReason()?.let { collision ->
                    emitEpisodeFailureNotice("cashout_collision", "Cash Out Blocked", collision)
                    return
                }
                if (uiTargets.gamePhase == "CRASHED" || uiTargets.gamePhase == "BETTING_OPEN") {
                    return
                }
                if (uiTargets.isCashOutBtnDetected && !uiTargets.isCashOutBtnEnabled) {
                    return
                }
            }

            val verifiedCashBounds = uiTargets?.cashOutButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }
            val targetCoords = verifiedCashBounds?.let { it.centerX().toFloat() to it.centerY().toFloat() }

            val gs = gameDetection.gameState.value
            val activeGameName = gs.displayedGameName.ifBlank { "Unknown" }
            val activeGameId = gs.gameId.ifBlank { "unknown" }
            val activeRoundId = (gs.currentRoundId ?: gs.latestRoundId)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "Unknown"
            val cashOutDetails = "game=$activeGameName ($activeGameId), round=$activeRoundId, target=${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", effectiveTarget)}"
            val nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val actionId = "cashout_${nowMs}_${(1000..9999).random()}"

            cashOutAttemptedThisRound = true
            cashOutAttemptCount += 1
            lastCashOutAttemptWallMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val initialRecord = ActionExecutionRecord(
                actionId = actionId,
                operationName = "CASH_OUT",
                state = ActionExecutionState.EXECUTING_ACTION,
                startTimestampMs = nowMs,
                targetCoordinates = targetCoords,
                details = cashOutDetails
            )
            _executionState.value = ActionExecutionState.EXECUTING_ACTION
            _executionRecord.value = initialRecord
            gameDetection.updateExecutionRecord(initialRecord)
            recordActivityLog(actionId, ActionExecutionState.EXECUTING_ACTION, "CASH_OUT", targetCoords, null, "Target ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", currentMultiplier)} reached ($cashOutDetails)", null, null)

            if (targetCoords != null) {
                onGestureDispatchRequested?.invoke(targetCoords.first, targetCoords.second, "CASH OUT")
            }
            FloatingHandsOverlayManager.pulsePassThroughAll(240L)

            lastLifecycleNoticeStage = "CASHING_OUT"
            emitExecutionNotice(
                type = StrategyNotificationType.AUTOMATION_ACTION,
                title = "Cashing Out at ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", currentMultiplier)}",
                message = "Target ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", effectiveTarget)} reached on $activeGameName (Round $activeRoundId).",
                occurrenceKey = "cashing_out_${activeRoundId}_${actionId}",
                priority = NotificationPriorityLevel.STANDARD,
                cooldownMs = 0L
            )

            targetUiInteractor.clickCashOut(targetCoords?.first, targetCoords?.second) { clickOk ->
                if (!clickOk) {
                    val failReason = "Cash-out gesture/click failed at ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", currentMultiplier)}."
                    val failedRecord = initialRecord.copy(
                        state = ActionExecutionState.FAILED,
                        completionTimestampMs = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                        isSuccess = false,
                        gestureDispatched = false,
                        failureReason = failReason
                    )
                    _executionRecord.value = failedRecord
                    _executionState.value = ActionExecutionState.FAILED
                    gameDetection.updateExecutionRecord(failedRecord)
                    recordActivityLog(actionId, ActionExecutionState.FAILED, "CASH_OUT", targetCoords, false, cashOutDetails, false, failReason)
                    publishStatusTicker(failReason)
                    emitEpisodeFailureNotice("cashout_click", "Cash Out Failed", failReason)
                    // A failed click is not an attempt that "used up" the round: allow a bounded retry.
                    cashOutAttemptedThisRound = false
                    return@clickCashOut
                }

                fun confirmCashOutSuccess() {
                    currentRoundCashedOut = true
                    gameDetection.setBetPlaced(false)

                    val completedRecord = initialRecord.copy(
                        state = ActionExecutionState.COMPLETED,
                        completionTimestampMs = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                        isSuccess = true,
                        gestureDispatched = true,
                        interfaceResponded = true,
                        stateConfirmed = true
                    )
                    _executionRecord.value = completedRecord
                    _executionState.value = ActionExecutionState.COMPLETED
                    gameDetection.updateExecutionRecord(completedRecord)
                    recordActivityLog(actionId, ActionExecutionState.COMPLETED, "CASH_OUT", targetCoords, true, "Cash out confirmed ($cashOutDetails)", true, null)
                    publishStatusTicker("Cashed out at ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", currentMultiplier)}")

                    StrategyEngine.triggerAction(
                        AutomationActionEvent(
                            actionType = ActionType.CASH_OUT,
                            amountOrMultiplier = currentMultiplier,
                            reason = "In-Flight Target Reached: ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", currentMultiplier)}x >= ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", effectiveTarget)}x"
                        )
                    )
                    val stakeDisplay = if (currentRoundStake > 0.0) {
                        CurrencyManager.formatAmount(currentRoundStake)
                    } else {
                        "manual bet"
                    }
                    lastLifecycleNoticeStage = "CASHOUT_CONFIRMED"
                    emitExecutionNotice(
                        type = StrategyNotificationType.AUTOMATION_ACTION,
                        title = "Cash Out Confirmed (${String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", currentMultiplier)})",
                        message = "Cashed out $stakeDisplay on $activeGameName (Round $activeRoundId).",
                        occurrenceKey = "cashout_ok_${actionId}",
                        priority = NotificationPriorityLevel.STANDARD,
                        cooldownMs = 0L
                    )
                }

                if (targetUiInteractor !is AccessibilityTargetUiInteractor ||
                    gameDetection.gameState.value.siteButtonState != SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE
                ) {
                    confirmCashOutSuccess()
                } else {
                    _executionState.value = ActionExecutionState.VERIFYING_ACTION
                    val verifyingRecord = initialRecord.copy(
                        state = ActionExecutionState.VERIFYING_ACTION,
                        gestureDispatched = true,
                        interfaceResponded = true
                    )
                    _executionRecord.value = verifyingRecord
                    gameDetection.updateExecutionRecord(verifyingRecord)

                    scope.launch {
                        var elapsed = 0L
                        var confirmed = false
                        while (elapsed < 2000L) {
                            delay(100L)
                            elapsed += 100L
                            val rescan = targetUiInteractor.refreshLiveTargets(null)
                            val btnState = rescan?.siteButtonState ?: gameDetection.gameState.value.siteButtonState
                            if (btnState != SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE) {
                                confirmed = true
                                break
                            }
                        }
                        if (confirmed) {
                            confirmCashOutSuccess()
                        } else {
                            val failReason = "Cash-out verification timed out (Cash Out button still visible)."
                            val failedRecord = initialRecord.copy(
                                state = ActionExecutionState.FAILED,
                                completionTimestampMs = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                                isSuccess = false,
                                gestureDispatched = true,
                                interfaceResponded = false,
                                stateConfirmed = false,
                                failureReason = failReason
                            )
                            _executionRecord.value = failedRecord
                            _executionState.value = ActionExecutionState.FAILED
                            gameDetection.updateExecutionRecord(failedRecord)
                            recordActivityLog(actionId, ActionExecutionState.FAILED, "CASH_OUT", targetCoords, true, cashOutDetails, false, failReason)
                            publishStatusTicker("Cash-out not confirmed by site")
                            emitEpisodeFailureNotice("cashout_verify", "Cash Out Unconfirmed", failReason)
                            cashOutAttemptedThisRound = false
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun formatTriggerLabel(trigger: StrategyTrigger, state: StrategyState): String? {
        return when (trigger) {
            is StrategyTrigger.LossRecoveryFired -> "${trigger.ruleConfig.dynamicTitle()} (Fetch ${trigger.ruleConfig.targetWinsToFetch} wins)"
            is StrategyTrigger.RangeWaitStarted -> "${trigger.ruleConfig.dynamicTitle()} (Waiting)"
            is StrategyTrigger.WaitTimerCompleted -> "${trigger.ruleConfig.dynamicTitle()} Ready"
            is StrategyTrigger.SessionTimedOut -> "3m Guard Timed Out (${trigger.previousRuleName})"
            is StrategyTrigger.Strategy1Fired -> "Strategy 1 Triggered (${trigger.consecutiveLowRounds}x low)"
            is StrategyTrigger.Strategy2Fired -> "Strategy 2 Triggered (>10x + ${trigger.waitRoundsCompleted} rounds)"
            is StrategyTrigger.HighMultiplierLossTrigger -> ">13.00x Doubled Stake (${CurrencyManager.formatCompact(trigger.doubledStake)})"
            StrategyTrigger.None -> state.activeRuleLabel?.let { "$it (${state.consecutiveWinsInSession}/${state.targetWinsToFetch} wins)" }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.data.model

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey

enum class StakeProgressionMode(val label: String) {
    DISABLED("Disabled (Flat Stake)"),
    ADD_AMOUNT("Add Amount"),
    DOUBLE_AMOUNT("Double Amount (2x)")
}

enum class StrategyCategory(val title: String) {
    STANDARD_RECOVERY("Standard Recovery Strategies"),
    MULTIPLIER_WAIT("Multiplier-Range Waiting Strategies"),
    QUICK_RECOVERY("Quick Recovery Strategies")
}

enum class StrategyRuleId(
    val displayName: String,
    val description: String,
    val category: StrategyCategory,
    val requiredLosses: Int = 0,
    val waitDurationMs: Long = 0L,
    val minRangeExclusive: Double = 0.0,
    val maxRangeExclusive: Double = 0.0,
    val defaultEnabled: Boolean = false
) {
    AFTER_9_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 9 Lows",
        description = "Observe consecutive crashes below threshold",
        category = StrategyCategory.STANDARD_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 9,
        defaultEnabled = true
    ),
    AFTER_14_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 14 Lows",
        description = "Observe 14 consecutive crashes below threshold",
        category = StrategyCategory.STANDARD_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 14,
        defaultEnabled = true
    ),
    RANGE_10_TO_100_WAIT_10M(
        displayName = "10x – 100x · 10m Window",
        description = "Track interval window after high multiplier",
        category = StrategyCategory.MULTIPLIER_WAIT,
        waitDurationMs = 10 * 60_000L,
        minRangeExclusive = 10.0,
        maxRangeExclusive = 100.0,
        defaultEnabled = true
    ),
    RANGE_100_TO_1000_WAIT_5M(
        displayName = "100x – 1000x · 5m Window",
        description = "Track interval window after ultra-high multiplier",
        category = StrategyCategory.MULTIPLIER_WAIT,
        waitDurationMs = 5 * 60_000L,
        minRangeExclusive = 100.0,
        maxRangeExclusive = 1000.0,
        defaultEnabled = true
    ),
    AFTER_2_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 2 Lows",
        description = "Observe 2 consecutive low crashes",
        category = StrategyCategory.QUICK_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 2,
        defaultEnabled = false
    ),
    AFTER_3_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 3 Lows",
        description = "Observe 3 consecutive low crashes",
        category = StrategyCategory.QUICK_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 3,
        defaultEnabled = false
    ),
    AFTER_5_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 5 Lows",
        description = "Observe 5 consecutive low crashes",
        category = StrategyCategory.QUICK_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 5,
        defaultEnabled = false
    ),
    AFTER_7_LOSSES(
        displayName = "After 7 Lows",
        description = "Observe 7 consecutive low crashes",
        category = StrategyCategory.QUICK_RECOVERY,
        requiredLosses = 7,
        defaultEnabled = false
    )
}

data class StrategyRuleConfig(
    val ruleId: StrategyRuleId,
    val isEnabled: Boolean = ruleId.defaultEnabled,
    val progressionMode: StakeProgressionMode = StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED,
    val customAddAmount: Double = 10.0,
    val targetWinsToFetch: Int = 2,
    val requiredLosses: Int = ruleId.requiredLosses,
    val waitMinutes: Int = (ruleId.waitDurationMs / 60_000L).toInt(),
    val minMultiplier: Double = ruleId.minRangeExclusive,
    val maxMultiplier: Double = ruleId.maxRangeExclusive
) {
    val waitDurationMs: Long get() = waitMinutes.coerceAtLeast(0) * 60_000L

    fun dynamicTitle(): String {
        return when {
            ruleId.category == StrategyCategory.MULTIPLIER_WAIT ->
                "${formatShortMult(minMultiplier)}–${formatShortMult(maxMultiplier)} · ${waitMinutes}m"
            else -> "After $requiredLosses Lows"
        }
    }

    private fun formatShortMult(v: Double): String {
        return if (v % 1.0 == 0.0) "${v.toLong()}x" else "${v}x"
    }
}

@Entity(tableName = "strategy_configs")
data class StrategyConfig(
    @PrimaryKey
    val id: Int = 1,
    val isStrategy1Enabled: Boolean = true,
    val isStrategy2Enabled: Boolean = false,
    val isHighMultiplierLossRuleEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val strategy1LowThreshold: Double = 2.00,
    val strategy1RequiredLowRounds: Int = 14,
    val strategy2HighThreshold: Double = 10.00,
    val strategy2WaitRounds: Int = 15,
    val baseStake: Double = 10.0,
    val multiplierProgression: Double = 2.0,
    val stopAfterConsecutiveWins: Int = 2,
    val targetMultiplier: Double = 2.00,
    val isAutoBetEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val isAutoCashOutEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val sessionTimeoutMinutes: Int = 3,
    val currencyPreference: String = "AUTO",
    val keepScreenAwake: Boolean = true,
    val diagnosticToastsEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val diagnosticNotificationsEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val scheduleEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val scheduleStartHour: Int = 8,
    val scheduleStartMinute: Int = 0,
    val scheduleEndHour: Int = 23,
    val scheduleEndMinute: Int = 0,
    // AutoPredictor statistical thresholds & bounded grid settings
    val lowThreshold: Double = 2.00,
    val boundaryValue: Double = 2.00,
    val bigThreshold: Double = 10.00,
    val gridRows: Int = 5, // 4 or 5
    val gridColumns: Int = 8, // 7 or 8
    val monitoringEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val pixelColorCaptureEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val ruleConfigsJson: String = serializeRuleConfigs(defaultRuleConfigs()),
    val maxStrategyBetAttempts: Int = 2,
    val isMartingaleEnabled: Boolean = false,
    // Master loss progression controls — override Martingale & per-strategy progression rules
    val lossProgressionEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val lossProgressionMode: StakeProgressionMode = StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED,
    val lossProgressionAddAmount: Double = 10.0,
    // Centralized Monitoring & Game Recognition settings
    val minDetectionConfidence: Float = 0.5f,
    val historyCollectionEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val autoGameRecognitionEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val preferredGameId: String = "ALL",
    // Centralized Automation & Verified Execution settings
    val automationMasterEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs: Long = 1200L,
    val maxBetRetries: Int = 2,
    val retryDelayMs: Long = 350L,
    val bettingWindowExpiryMs: Long = 6000L,
    val dismissKeyboardBeforeBet: Boolean = true,
    val verifyStakeReadBack: Boolean = true,
    val verifyButtonStateBeforeAction: Boolean = true,
    // Game-side Auto Cash Out & Betting-Window Step Timing settings
    val gameAutoCashOutEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val gameAutoCashOutTarget: Double = 2.00,
    val stakeEditTimeoutMs: Long = 500L,
    val autoCashOutTimeoutMs: Long = 500L,
    val betWindowTotalTimeoutMs: Long = 3000L,
    val minRemainingBetWindowMs: Long = 1200L,
    // Your own stake limits (0 = no limit). The site's min/max is not shown on the main game screen.
    val minStake: Double = 0.0,
    val maxStake: Double = 0.0,
    // true = also auto cash out a bet you placed by hand (any visible Cash Out button); false = only bets this app placed.
    val cashOutUntrackedBets: Boolean = true
) {
    fun resolvedMasterProgressionMode(): StakeProgressionMode {
        return if (lossProgressionEnabled && lossProgressionMode != StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED) {
            lossProgressionMode
        } else {
            StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED
        }
    }

    fun getRuleConfigs(): List<StrategyRuleConfig> = deserializeRuleConfigs(ruleConfigsJson)

    fun getRuleConfig(ruleId: StrategyRuleId): StrategyRuleConfig =
        getRuleConfigs().firstOrNull { it.ruleId == ruleId }
            ?: StrategyRuleConfig(ruleId = ruleId)

    fun withUpdatedRuleConfigs(configs: List<StrategyRuleConfig>): StrategyConfig {
        return copy(ruleConfigsJson = serializeRuleConfigs(configs))
    }

    fun withUpdatedRule(updated: StrategyRuleConfig): StrategyConfig {
        val current = getRuleConfigs().toMutableList()
        val index = current.indexOfFirst { it.ruleId == updated.ruleId }
        if (index >= 0) {
            current[index] = updated
        } else {
            current.add(updated)
        }
        return withUpdatedRuleConfigs(current)
    }

    companion object {
        fun defaultRuleConfigs(): List<StrategyRuleConfig> =
            StrategyRuleId.entries.map { ruleId ->
                StrategyRuleConfig(
                    ruleId = ruleId,
                    isEnabled = ruleId.defaultEnabled,
                    progressionMode = StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED,
                    customAddAmount = 10.0,
                    targetWinsToFetch = 2,
                    requiredLosses = ruleId.requiredLosses,
                    waitMinutes = (ruleId.waitDurationMs / 60_000L).toInt(),
                    minMultiplier = ruleId.minRangeExclusive,
                    maxMultiplier = ruleId.maxRangeExclusive
                )
            }

        fun serializeRuleConfigs(configs: List<StrategyRuleConfig>): String {
            return configs.joinToString(";") { rule ->
                listOf(
                    rule.ruleId.name,
                    rule.isEnabled,
                    rule.progressionMode.name,
                    rule.customAddAmount,
                    rule.targetWinsToFetch,
                    rule.requiredLosses,
                    rule.waitMinutes,
                    rule.minMultiplier,
                    rule.maxMultiplier
                ).joinToString("|")
            }
        }

        fun deserializeRuleConfigs(encoded: String?): List<StrategyRuleConfig> {
            val defaultsById = defaultRuleConfigs().associateBy { it.ruleId }.toMutableMap()
            if (encoded.isNullOrBlank()) {
                return StrategyRuleId.entries.mapNotNull { defaultsById[it] }
            }
            encoded.split(";").forEach { entry ->
                val parts = entry.split("|")
                if (parts.size >= 5) {
                    val ruleId = runCatching { StrategyRuleId.valueOf(parts[0]) }.getOrNull()
                    if (ruleId != null) {
                        val isEnabled = parts[1].toBooleanStrictOrNull() ?: ruleId.defaultEnabled
                        val mode = runCatching { StakeProgressionMode.valueOf(parts[2]) }
                            .getOrDefault(StakeProgressionMode.DISABLED)
                        val addAmount = parts[3].toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 10.0
                        val targetWins = parts[4].toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1) ?: 2
                        val reqLosses = if (ruleId.category == StrategyCategory.MULTIPLIER_WAIT) {
                            0
                        } else {
                            parts.getOrNull(5)?.toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1) ?: ruleId.requiredLosses
                        }
                        val waitMins = parts.getOrNull(6)?.toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(0)
                            ?: (ruleId.waitDurationMs / 60_000L).toInt()
                        val minMult = parts.getOrNull(7)?.toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: ruleId.minRangeExclusive
                        val maxMult = parts.getOrNull(8)?.toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: ruleId.maxRangeExclusive

                        defaultsById[ruleId] = StrategyRuleConfig(
                            ruleId = ruleId,
                            isEnabled = isEnabled,
                            progressionMode = mode,
                            customAddAmount = addAmount,
                            targetWinsToFetch = targetWins,
                            requiredLosses = reqLosses,
                            waitMinutes = waitMins,
                            minMultiplier = minMult,
                            maxMultiplier = maxMult
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            return StrategyRuleId.entries.mapNotNull { defaultsById[it] }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.service

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService

data class DetectedUiTargets(
    val detectedMultiplier: Double? = null,
    val ribbonMultipliers: List<Double> = emptyList(),
    val ribbonItems: List<com.example.data.engine.ExtractedHistoryItem> = emptyList(),
    val historyPanelExpanded: Boolean = false,
    val hasExpandableHistoryControl: Boolean = false,
    val hasScrollableHistoryContainer: Boolean = false,
    val gamePhase: String = "WAITING_FOR_GAME", // "WAITING_FOR_GAME", "BETTING_OPEN", "IN_FLIGHT", "CRASHED", "LIVE_DETECTION_LOST"
    val siteButtonState: SiteButtonState = SiteButtonState.UNKNOWN,
    val isFlewAwayDetected: Boolean = false,
    val isWaitingForNextRound: Boolean = false,
    val isCancelBtnDetected: Boolean = false,
    val detectedUrl: String? = null,
    val detectedCurrentRoundId: String? = null,
    val pageTextHints: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val visibleRawTitles: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val liveMultiplierBounds: Rect? = null,
    val stakeInputBounds: Rect? = null,
    val doubleButtonBounds: Rect? = null,
    val addButtonBounds: Rect? = null,
    val betButtonBounds: Rect? = null,
    val cancelButtonBounds: Rect? = null,
    val cashOutButtonBounds: Rect? = null,
    val detectedStakeText: String? = null,
    val detectedRoundId: String? = detectedCurrentRoundId,
    val roundId: String? = detectedCurrentRoundId,
    val detectedBalance: Double? = null,
    val detectedCurrency: String? = null,
    // Verified automation, OCR fusion & game-side Auto Cash Out fields
    val scannedAtMs: Long = 0L,
    val foregroundPackage: String = "",
    val countdownRemainingMs: Long? = null,
    val isBetBtnEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val isCashOutBtnEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val isCancelBtnEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val isStakeInputEditable: Boolean = true,
    val autoCashOutToggleBounds: Rect? = null,
    val isAutoCashOutToggleChecked: Boolean? = null,
    val autoCashOutInputBounds: Rect? = null,
    val detectedAutoCashOutText: String? = null,
    val detectedAutoCashOutValue: Double? = null,
    val conflictReasons: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val ocrVerified: Boolean = false,
    val fusedConfidence: Float = 0f,
    val canQueueBet: Boolean = false,
    val autoCashOutTabBounds: Rect? = null,
    val isAutoCashOutRowVisible: Boolean = false
) {
    val isStakeInputDetected: Boolean get() = stakeInputBounds != null
    val isDoubleBtnDetected: Boolean get() = doubleButtonBounds != null
    val isAddBtnDetected: Boolean get() = addButtonBounds != null
    val isBetBtnDetected: Boolean get() = betButtonBounds != null
    val isCashOutBtnDetected: Boolean get() = cashOutButtonBounds != null
    val isAutoCashOutControlDetected: Boolean get() = autoCashOutToggleBounds != null || autoCashOutInputBounds != null

    /**
     * Ensures a BET tap target cannot overlap or strike Double (2x), Cancel, Auto Cash Out,
     * Stake Input, or an active/distinct Cash Out button.
     */
    fun detectBetCollisionReason(): String? {
        val betRect = betButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty } ?: return "BET button bounds missing or empty"
        doubleButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { dbl ->
            if (Rect.intersects(betRect, dbl)) {
                return "BET bounds collide with 2x/Double control"
            }
        }
        stakeInputBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { stk ->
            if (Rect.intersects(betRect, stk)) {
                return "BET bounds collide with Stake input control"
            }
        }
        autoCashOutToggleBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { tgl ->
            if (Rect.intersects(betRect, tgl)) {
                return "BET bounds collide with Auto Cash Out toggle"
            }
        }
        autoCashOutInputBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { ain ->
            if (Rect.intersects(betRect, ain)) {
                return "BET bounds collide with Auto Cash Out target input"
            }
        }
        if (isCancelBtnDetected || siteButtonState == SiteButtonState.BET_QUEUED) {
            cancelButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { cnl ->
                if (Rect.intersects(betRect, cnl)) {
                    return "BET bounds collide with active CANCEL control"
                }
            }
        }
        if (siteButtonState == SiteButtonState.CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE) {
            return "Site button is CASH_OUT_AVAILABLE, not BET"
        }
        cashOutButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { co ->
            if (co != betRect && Rect.intersects(betRect, co)) {
                return "BET bounds collide with distinct CASH OUT control"
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    fun detectCashOutCollisionReason(): String? {
        val coRect = cashOutButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty } ?: return "CASH OUT button bounds missing or empty"
        doubleButtonBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { dbl ->
            if (Rect.intersects(coRect, dbl)) return "CASH OUT bounds collide with 2x/Double control"
        }
        autoCashOutToggleBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { tgl ->
            if (Rect.intersects(coRect, tgl)) return "CASH OUT bounds collide with Auto Cash Out toggle"
        }
        autoCashOutInputBounds?.takeIf { !it.isEmpty }?.let { ain ->
            if (Rect.intersects(coRect, ain)) return "CASH OUT bounds collide with Auto Cash Out input"
        }
        return null
    }
}

/** A switch-like or editable control found while scanning; matched to its label by row geometry. */

)

data class HierarchyCandidate(

)

data class NodeHierarchyReport(

    val totalNodesVisited: Int,
    val truncated: Boolean,
    val lines: List<String>,
    val candidates: List<HierarchyCandidate>
) {
    fun toFormattedText(): String = buildString {
        lines.forEach { line ->
            appendLine(line)
        }
    }
}

/**
 * AccessibilityController - Crash-safe AccessibilityService extending Android accessibility
 * to read browser URL bars (BetPawa vs SportyBet), separate top history ribbons from live center
 * multipliers, track real site Bet/Cancel/Cash Out button states, edit stakes, dispatch gestures
 * cleanly on the Main Looper without overlays blocking their own taps, and log node hierarchies.
 */
open class AccessibilityController : AccessibilityService() {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AccessibilityController"
        const val NODE_HIERARCHY_TAG = "A11yNodeTree"
        const val DEFAULT_HIERARCHY_LOG_THROTTLE_MS = 1200L
        const val FORCE_HIERARCHY_REFRESH_MS = 6000L
        private const val MAX_TREE_DEPTH = 64
        private const val MAX_LOGGED_NODES = 600
        private const val MAX_SCAN_NODES = 1800

        /** "Bet 400.00 TZS" / "Bet\n1.00 TZS" - the real BET button (name followed by an amount). */
        private val BET_BUTTON_WITH_AMOUNT = Regex("^bet\\s*[0-9][0-9.,]*(\\s.*)?$", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL)

        /** A bare "Bet" label only counts as the button when at least this fraction of the screen wide (tabs are narrower). */
        private const val BET_BUTTON_MIN_WIDTH_FRACTION = 0.35f

        /** Max vertical distance (as a multiple of the label height) for a control to count as "on the same row". */
        private const val SAME_ROW_TOLERANCE = 1.0f

        var controllerInstance: AccessibilityController? = null
            protected set

        fun isControllerConnected(): Boolean = controllerInstance != null

        /**
         * Callback notified before/after automated gesture dispatch so ScreenEarnerOverlayService
         * temporarily sets FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE on floating hands/panels and never intercepts its own tap.
         */
        @Volatile
        var gestureOverlayPassThroughListener: ((isDispatching: Boolean) -> Unit)? = null

        fun notifyOverlayPassThrough(isDispatching: Boolean) {
            runCatching { gestureOverlayPassThroughListener?.invoke(isDispatching) }
            runCatching { FloatingHandsOverlayManager.setAllPassThrough(isDispatching) }
        }

        private val _multiplierFlow = MutableStateFlow<Double?>(null)
        val multiplierFlow: StateFlow<Double?> = _multiplierFlow.asStateFlow()

        private val _detectedTargets = MutableStateFlow(DetectedUiTargets())
        val detectedTargets: StateFlow<DetectedUiTargets> = _detectedTargets.asStateFlow()

        private val _nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled = MutableStateFlow(true)
        val nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled.asStateFlow()

        private val _lastLoggedHierarchy = MutableStateFlow("")
        val lastLoggedHierarchy: StateFlow<String> = _lastLoggedHierarchy.asStateFlow()

        private const val UI_EMIT_THROTTLE_MS = 115L
        private const val MIN_PASS_THROUGH_MS = 100L

        private val _diagnosticMessage = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
        val diagnosticMessage: StateFlow<String?> = _diagnosticMessage.asStateFlow()

        fun emitDiagnostic(message: String) {
            _diagnosticMessage.value = message
        }

        // Match decimal multipliers like 1.00x, 12.80x, 140.25x (avoids matching "2x" double-stake button)
        val MULTIPLIER_PATTERN: Pattern = Pattern.compile("(\\d+\\.\\d{1,2})\\s*x", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)

        private val BALANCE_SUFFIX_PATTERN: Pattern = Pattern.compile(
            "([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\\.[0-9]{1,2})?)\\s*(TZS|TSH|ZMW|KES|KSH|UGX|NGN|GHS|USD)",
            Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE
        )

        private val BALANCE_PREFIX_PATTERN: Pattern = Pattern.compile(
            "(TZS|TSH|ZMW|KES|KSH|UGX|NGN|GHS|USD|₦|GH₵|\\$)\\s*([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\\.[0-9]{1,2})?)",
            Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE
        )

        private val NUMERIC_VALUE_PATTERN: Pattern = Pattern.compile("([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\\.[0-9]{1,2})?)")

        private val URL_HINT_PATTERN: Pattern = Pattern.compile(
            "(?:https?://)?[a-z0-9.-]*(?:betpawa|sportybet)[a-z0-9.-]*(?:/[^\\s]*)?|(?:https?://)?[a-z0-9.-]+\\.(?:com|co\\.[a-z]{2}|ng|ke|tz|zm|gh|ug|za|mw|rw|bet)(?:/[^\\s]*)?",
            Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE
        )

        fun setNodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
            _nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled.value = enabled
        }

        fun formatStakeForInput(stake: Double): String {
            return if (stake % 1.0 == 0.0) {
                stake.toLong().toString()
            } else {
                String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", stake)
            }
        }

        /**
         * Parses account balance + currency code from a header node (e.g., "10.18 TZS", "ZMW 450.00").
         * Ignores stake inputs and multiplier labels ending with 'x'.
         */
        fun parseBalanceCandidate(
            text: String,
            lowerId: String = "",
            bounds: Rect = Rect(),
            viewportHeightPx: Int = 0
        ): Pair<Double, String?>? {
            if (text.isBlank()) return null
            val lowerText = text.lowercase(Locale.US)
            if (lowerText.contains("stake") || lowerText.contains("wager") || lowerId.contains("stake") ||
                lowerId.contains("url_bar") || lowerId.contains("omnibox") ||
                lowerText.contains("auto") || lowerText.contains("cash out") || lowerText.contains("cashout")
            ) {
                return null
            }
            if (text.trim().endsWith("x", ignoreCase = true) && MULTIPLIER_PATTERN.matcher(text).find()) {
                return null
            }

            val suffixMatcher = BALANCE_SUFFIX_PATTERN.matcher(text)
            if (suffixMatcher.find()) {
                val amount = suffixMatcher.group(1)?.replace(",", "")?.toDoubleOrNull()
                val rawCurr = suffixMatcher.group(2)?.uppercase(Locale.US)
                val normCurr = when (rawCurr) {
                    "TSH" -> "TZS"
                    "KSH" -> "KES"
                    else -> rawCurr
                }
                if (amount != null && amount.isFinite() && amount >= 0.0) {
                    return amount to normCurr
                }
            }

            val prefixMatcher = BALANCE_PREFIX_PATTERN.matcher(text)
            if (prefixMatcher.find()) {
                val rawCurr = prefixMatcher.group(1)?.uppercase(Locale.US)
                val amount = prefixMatcher.group(2)?.replace(",", "")?.toDoubleOrNull()
                val normCurr = when (rawCurr) {
                    "TSH" -> "TZS"
                    "KSH" -> "KES"
                    "₦" -> "NGN"
                    "GH₵" -> "GHS"
                    "$" -> "USD"
                    else -> rawCurr
                }
                if (amount != null && amount.isFinite() && amount >= 0.0) {
                    return amount to normCurr
                }
            }

            // Explicit balance/wallet node without currency code in string, provided it sits in header area or ID
            val inHeaderBand = bounds.isEmpty || viewportHeightPx <= 0 || bounds.top <= (viewportHeightPx * 0.28f).toInt()
            val hasBalanceHint = lowerId.contains("balance") || lowerId.contains("wallet") ||
                lowerId.contains("credit") || lowerText.contains("balance") || lowerText.contains("bal:")
            if (hasBalanceHint && inHeaderBand) {
                val numMatcher = NUMERIC_VALUE_PATTERN.matcher(text)
                if (numMatcher.find()) {
                    val amount = numMatcher.group(1)?.replace(",", "")?.toDoubleOrNull()
                    if (amount != null && amount.isFinite() && amount >= 0.0) {
                        val detectedCurr = CurrencyManager.parseCurrencyCodeFromText(text)?.code
                        return amount to detectedCurr
                    }
                }
            }
            return null
        }

        fun verifyStakeAccepted(readBackText: String?, expectedStake: Double): Boolean {
            if (readBackText.isNullOrBlank()) return false
            val cleaned = readBackText.replace(",", "").trim()
            val parsed = cleaned.toDoubleOrNull()
                ?: NUMERIC_VALUE_PATTERN.matcher(cleaned).let { m ->
                    if (m.find()) m.group(1)?.toDoubleOrNull() else null
                }
                ?: return false
            return abs(parsed - expectedStake) <= 0.05
        }

        /**
         * Separates the top horizontal history ribbon (`15.74x  2.00x  8.14x ...`) from the prominent
         * central live multiplier (`1.27x`, `3.01x`), using Y-band clustering, container hints,
         * and bounding-box dimensions so ribbon items are never mistaken for the live multiplier.
         *
         * @return Pair(ribbonMultipliersLeftToRight, liveCenterMultiplier)
         */
        fun extractRibbonAndCenterMultipliers(
            candidates: List<MultiplierCandidate>,
            viewportHeightPx: Int = 0
        ): Pair<List<Double>, Double?> {
            val (items, center) = extractRibbonItemsAndCenterMultiplier(candidates, viewportHeightPx)
            return items.map { it.multiplier } to center
        }

        fun extractRibbonItemsAndCenterMultiplier(
            candidates: List<MultiplierCandidate>,
            viewportHeightPx: Int = 0,
            maxCenterY: Int = Int.MAX_VALUE
        ): Pair<List<com.example.data.engine.ExtractedHistoryItem>, Double?> {
            val valid = candidates.filter { it.value >= 1.0 }
            if (valid.isEmpty()) return emptyList<com.example.data.engine.ExtractedHistoryItem>() to null

            val explicitHistory = valid.filter { it.isHistoryHinted }.toMutableSet()
            val positioned = valid.filter { !it.bounds.isEmpty }

            // Expanded grids (e.g. SportyBet 8-column Round History modal) can occupy upper ~60% of the screen.
            val topCutoffY = if (viewportHeightPx > 0) {
                (viewportHeightPx * 0.62f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(220)
            } else {
                val maxBottom = positioned.maxOfOrNull { it.bounds.bottom } ?: 0
                if (maxBottom > 0) (maxBottom * 0.65f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(220) else Int.MAX_VALUE
            }

            // Collect horizontal rows in topCutoffY so single-row ribbons AND multi-row Round History grids
            // retain all rows cleanly without modulo division artifacts dropping row-head items (e.g. 1.53x).
            val horizontalRows = mutableListOf<List<MultiplierCandidate>>()
            for (anchor in positioned) {
                val anchorCy = anchor.bounds.centerY()
                if (anchorCy > topCutoffY) continue
                val rowMatches = positioned.filter { other ->
                    other.bounds.centerY() <= topCutoffY &&
                        abs(other.bounds.centerY() - anchorCy) <= 45
                }
                if (rowMatches.size >= 2) {
                    horizontalRows.add(rowMatches)
                }
            }

            // Determine typical compact chip height from detected rows
            val medianRowHeight = horizontalRows.flatten()
                .map { it.bounds.height() }
                .filter { it > 0 }
                .sorted()
                .let { heights ->
                    if (heights.isEmpty()) 48 else heights[heights.size / 2]
                }

            val gridBandCandidates = horizontalRows
                .flatten()
                .distinctBy { it.orderIndex }
                .filter { c ->
                    c.bounds.height() <= (medianRowHeight * 2.2f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(110)
                }

            val combinedRibbon = (explicitHistory + gridBandCandidates)
                .distinctBy { it.orderIndex }

            // Assign stable row indices by vertical center proximity rather than modulo division (top / 45)
            // so a 1px vertical jitter between neighboring badges in the same row never reverses left-to-right order.
            val sortedByY = combinedRibbon.sortedBy {
                if (it.bounds.isEmpty) Int.MAX_VALUE else it.bounds.centerY()
            }
            val rowGroups = mutableListOf<MutableList<MultiplierCandidate>>()
            val unpositioned = mutableListOf<MultiplierCandidate>()
            for (cand in sortedByY) {
                if (cand.bounds.isEmpty) {
                    unpositioned.add(cand)
                } else {
                    val lastGroup = rowGroups.lastOrNull()
                    if (lastGroup != null) {
                        val groupAvgCy = lastGroup.map { it.bounds.centerY() }.average()
                        val tolerance = (medianRowHeight * 0.8f).toInt().coerceIn(28, 64)
                        if (abs(cand.bounds.centerY() - groupAvgCy) <= tolerance) {
                            lastGroup.add(cand)
                            continue
                        }
                    }
                    rowGroups.add(mutableListOf(cand))
                }
            }

            val ribbonSet = rowGroups.flatMap { row ->
                row.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.bounds.left }, { it.orderIndex }))
            } + unpositioned.sortedBy { it.orderIndex }

            val sourceType = if (explicitHistory.size >= 8 || rowGroups.size >= 2) {
                com.example.data.model.CollectionSource.EXPANDED_PANEL
            } else {
                com.example.data.model.CollectionSource.VISIBLE_RIBBON
            }
            val extractedItems = ribbonSet.mapIndexed { idx, c ->
                val sampled = ScreenColorSampler.sampleColorInBounds(c.bounds)
                com.example.data.engine.ExtractedHistoryItem(
                    multiplier = c.value,
                    roundId = c.roundId,
                    gameTimestampMs = c.gameTimestampMs,
                    orderIndex = idx,
                    source = sourceType,
                    bounds = Rect(c.bounds),
                    detectedColorHex = sampled?.hex,
                    colorCategory = sampled?.category,
                    isPixelColorVerified = sampled?.isPixelVerified == true,
                    displayText = c.displayText
                )
            }

            val ribbonOrderIndices = ribbonSet.map { it.orderIndex }.toSet()
            val ribbonBottomY = ribbonSet.filter { !it.bounds.isEmpty }.maxOfOrNull { it.bounds.bottom } ?: Int.MIN_VALUE

            val nonRibbon = valid.filter { it.orderIndex !in ribbonOrderIndices }
            if (nonRibbon.isEmpty()) {
                return extractedItems to null
            }

            // Exclude any multiplier positioned below the game stage / controls (e.g. All Bets / My Bets / Auto Cash Out rows)
            val stageFiltered = nonRibbon.filter { cand ->
                if (cand.bounds.isEmpty) {
                    true
                } else {
                    val cy = cand.bounds.centerY()
                    val belowStageFloor = maxCenterY < Int.MAX_VALUE && cy >= maxCenterY
                    val isTopBarLoneChip = viewportHeightPx >= 800 &&
                        cy < (viewportHeightPx * 0.18f).toInt() &&
                        cand.bounds.height() <= 70
                    !belowStageFloor && !isTopBarLoneChip
                }
            }
            if (stageFiltered.isEmpty()) {
                return extractedItems to null
            }

            val belowRibbon = if (ribbonBottomY > Int.MIN_VALUE) {
                stageFiltered.filter { it.bounds.isEmpty || it.bounds.centerY() > ribbonBottomY }
            } else {
                stageFiltered
            }
            val centerPool = if (belowRibbon.isNotEmpty()) belowRibbon else stageFiltered

            val liveCenter = centerPool.maxWithOrNull(
                compareBy<MultiplierCandidate>(
                    { if (it.bounds.isEmpty) 0 else it.bounds.height() },
                    { if (it.bounds.isEmpty) 0 else it.bounds.width() * it.bounds.height() },
                    { -it.orderIndex }
                )
            )?.value

            return extractedItems to liveCenter
        }
    }

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val workerScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default + SupervisorJob())

    @Volatile
    private var hierarchyLogThrottleMs: Long = DEFAULT_HIERARCHY_LOG_THROTTLE_MS

    @Volatile
    private var lastHierarchyLogTimeMs: Long = 0L

    @Volatile
    private var lastHierarchySignature: Int = 0

    @Volatile
    private var lastEventScanTimeMs: Long = 0L

    @Volatile
    private var lastUiEmitTimeMs: Long = 0L

    @Volatile
    private var passThroughStartedAtMs: Long = 0L

    @Volatile
    private var pendingOverlayRestoreRunnable: Runnable? = null

    private val gestureGeneration = java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong(0L)
    @Volatile
    private var lastDispatchedCoords: Pair<Float, Float>? = null
    @Volatile
    private var lastDispatchedTimeMs: Long = 0L
    private val pendingGestureRunnables = java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArraySet<Runnable>()

    fun setHierarchyLogThrottleMs(throttleMs: Long) {
        hierarchyLogThrottleMs = throttleMs.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    }

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        try {
            super.onServiceConnected()
            controllerInstance = this
            safeLogD(TAG, "AccessibilityController connected. Node hierarchy logger active under tag '$NODE_HIERARCHY_TAG'.")
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            safeLogE(TAG, "Non-fatal error during onServiceConnected", t)
        }
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        try {
            if (event == null) return
            val ownPkg = runCatching { applicationContext?.packageName }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
            val eventPkg = try { event.packageName?.toString().orEmpty() } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
            // Ignore Screen Earner's own overlay/bubble/panel events before consuming scan throttle
            if (ownPkg.isNotBlank() && eventPkg == ownPkg) {
                return
            }

            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val eventType = try { event.eventType } catch (_: Throwable) { AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_CONTENT_CHANGED }

            // Throttle dense content-changed churn (~115ms) while allowing window state transitions through immediately
            val isWindowTransition = eventType == AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED ||
                eventType == AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOWS_CHANGED
            if (!isWindowTransition &&
                eventType == AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_CONTENT_CHANGED &&
                (now - lastEventScanTimeMs) < UI_EMIT_THROTTLE_MS
            ) {
                return
            }
            lastEventScanTimeMs = now

            val (rootNode, fromWindowsList) = resolveBestTargetWindowRoot() ?: return
            try {
                val rootPkg = safeNodePackageName(rootNode)
                if (ownPkg.isNotBlank() && rootPkg == ownPkg) {
                    return
                }
                val prevTargets = _detectedTargets.value
                val targets = scanWindowTargets(rootNode)

                val stateChanged = targets.siteButtonState != prevTargets.siteButtonState ||
                    targets.gamePhase != prevTargets.gamePhase ||
                    targets.isFlewAwayDetected != prevTargets.isFlewAwayDetected ||
                    targets.detectedUrl != prevTargets.detectedUrl ||
                    targets.detectedBalance != prevTargets.detectedBalance ||
                    targets.ribbonMultipliers != prevTargets.ribbonMultipliers

                if (isWindowTransition || stateChanged || (now - lastUiEmitTimeMs) >= UI_EMIT_THROTTLE_MS) {
                    lastUiEmitTimeMs = now
                    _detectedTargets.value = targets
                    if (targets.detectedMultiplier != null) {
                        _multiplierFlow.value = targets.detectedMultiplier
                    }
                }

                // Offload structural Logcat reporting checks off Main UI Thread where throttled
                val elapsedSinceLog = (now - lastHierarchyLogTimeMs).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                if (_nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled.value &&
                    (lastHierarchyLogTimeMs == 0L || elapsedSinceLog >= hierarchyLogThrottleMs ||
                        eventType == AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED)
                ) {
                    val pkg = safeNodePackageName(rootNode).ifBlank {
                        try { event.packageName?.toString().orEmpty() } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
                    }
                    val ownPkg = runCatching { applicationContext?.packageName }.getOrNull()
                    if (ownPkg.isNullOrBlank() || pkg != ownPkg) {
                        val report = buildNodeHierarchyReport(
                            rootNode = rootNode,
                            packageName = pkg,
                            eventLabel = runCatching { AccessibilityEvent.eventTypeToString(eventType) }.getOrDefault(eventType.toString()),
                            timestampMs = now
                        )
                        workerScope.launch {
                            try {
                                val sig = computeStructuralSignature(report)
                                if (lastHierarchyLogTimeMs == 0L ||
                                    (sig != lastHierarchySignature && elapsedSinceLog >= hierarchyLogThrottleMs) ||
                                    elapsedSinceLog >= FORCE_HIERARCHY_REFRESH_MS
                                ) {
                                    lastHierarchyLogTimeMs = now
                                    lastHierarchySignature = sig
                                    emitReportToLogcat(report)
                                }
                            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } finally {
                if (fromWindowsList) {
                    safeRecycleRoot(rootNode)
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            // Never allow unhandled exceptions to crash AccessibilityService and get it disabled by Android
            safeLogE(TAG, "Caught exception in onAccessibilityEvent", t)
        }
    }

    protected fun safeRootInActiveWindow(): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        return try {
            rootInActiveWindow
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            safeLogW(TAG, "Unable to acquire rootInActiveWindow: ${t.message}")
            null
        }
    }

    protected open fun safeInteractiveWindows(): List<AccessibilityWindowInfo> {
        return try {
            windows ?: emptyList()
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            safeLogW(TAG, "Unable to acquire windows list: ${t.message}")
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private fun safeRecycleRoot(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?) {
        if (node == null) return
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            try {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                node.recycle()
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
            }
        }
    }

    private fun isBettingOrBrowserPackage(pkg: String): Boolean {
        val lower = pkg.lowercase(Locale.US)
        return lower.contains("betpawa") ||
            lower.contains("sportybet") ||
            lower.contains("betway") ||
            lower.contains("1xbet") ||
            lower.contains("msport") ||
            lower.contains("betking") ||
            lower.contains("parimatch") ||
            lower.contains("aviator") ||
            lower.contains("spribe") ||
            lower.contains("chrome") ||
            lower.contains("chromium") ||
            lower.contains("browser") ||
            lower.contains("sbrowser") ||
            lower.contains("webview") ||
            lower.contains("firefox") ||
            lower.contains("opera") ||
            lower.contains("emmx") ||
            lower.contains("brave") ||
            lower.contains("kiwi") ||
            lower.contains("duckduckgo") ||
            lower.contains("vivaldi") ||
            lower.contains("yandex") ||
            lower.contains("ucmobile")
    }

    /**
     * Resolves the best Accessibility root node across single-window OR split-screen/multi-window
     * modes (`AccessibilityService.windows`), skipping Screen Earner's own overlay windows.
     */
    protected fun resolveBestTargetWindowRoot(): Pair<AccessibilityNodeInfo, Boolean>? {
        val ownPkg = runCatching { applicationContext?.packageName }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
        val activeRoot = safeRootInActiveWindow()
        val activePkg = activeRoot?.let { safeNodePackageName(it) }.orEmpty()
        val activeIsOwn = ownPkg.isNotBlank() && activePkg == ownPkg
        val activeIsSystemUi = activePkg == "com.android.systemui"

        if (activeRoot != null && !activeIsOwn && isBettingOrBrowserPackage(activePkg)) {
            return activeRoot to false
        }

        // Scan interactive windows to locate background/split-screen browser or betting window
        val winList = safeInteractiveWindows()
        var nonSelfFallback: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = null
        for (i in winList.indices.reversed()) {
            val winRoot = try { winList[i].root } catch (_: Throwable) { null } ?: continue
            val pkg = safeNodePackageName(winRoot)
            if (ownPkg.isNotBlank() && pkg == ownPkg) {
                safeRecycleRoot(winRoot)
                continue
            }
            if (isBettingOrBrowserPackage(pkg)) {
                safeRecycleRoot(nonSelfFallback)
                return winRoot to true
            }
            if (pkg != "com.android.systemui" && nonSelfFallback == null) {
                nonSelfFallback = winRoot
            } else {
                safeRecycleRoot(winRoot)
            }
        }

        if (nonSelfFallback != null) {
            return nonSelfFallback to true
        }
        return if (activeRoot != null && !activeIsOwn && !activeIsSystemUi) {
            activeRoot to false
        } else {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Immediately dumps the active window's accessibility node hierarchy to Logcat regardless of throttle.
     */
    fun forceDumpNodeHierarchy(): NodeHierarchyReport? {
        return try {
            val rootNode = safeRootInActiveWindow() ?: return null
            val pkg = safeNodePackageName(rootNode)
            logNodeHierarchyNow(
                rootNode = rootNode,
                packageName = pkg,
                eventLabel = "MANUAL_DUMP",
                currentTimeMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            safeLogE(TAG, "forceDumpNodeHierarchy failed", t)
            null
        }
    }

    protected open fun childNodesOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): List<AccessibilityNodeInfo> {
        val count = try {
            node.childCount.coerceAtLeast(0)
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            0
        }
        if (count == 0) return emptyList()
        val list = ArrayList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>(count)
        for (i in 0 until count) {
            val child = try {
                node.getChild(i)
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                null
            }
            if (child != null) {
                list.add(child)
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    protected fun safeRecycleNode(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, rootNode: AccessibilityNodeInfo) {
        if (node == null || node === rootNode) return
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            try {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                node.recycle()
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
            }
        }
    }

    private fun safeNodePackageName(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String {
        return try {
            node.packageName?.toString().orEmpty()
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            ""
        }
    }

    internal fun maybeLogNodeHierarchy(
        rootNode: AccessibilityNodeInfo,
        event: AccessibilityEvent?,
        currentTimeMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        skipOwnPackage: Boolean = true
    ): Boolean {
        if (!_nodeHierarchyLoggingEnabled.value) return false

        val pkg = safeNodePackageName(rootNode).ifBlank {
            try {
                event?.packageName?.toString().orEmpty()
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                ""
            }
        }
        val ownPkg = runCatching { applicationContext?.packageName }.getOrNull()
        if (skipOwnPackage && !ownPkg.isNullOrBlank() && pkg == ownPkg) {
            return false
        }

        val eventType = try {
            event?.eventType ?: AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_CONTENT_CHANGED
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_CONTENT_CHANGED
        }
        val eventLabel = try {
            AccessibilityEvent.eventTypeToString(eventType)
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            eventType.toString()
        }
        val report = buildNodeHierarchyReport(
            rootNode = rootNode,
            packageName = pkg,
            eventLabel = eventLabel,
            timestampMs = currentTimeMs
        )
        val signature = computeStructuralSignature(report)
