                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = stakeTimeoutText,
                            onValueChange = { stakeTimeoutText = it },
                            label = { Text("Stake Target", fontSize = 10.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_stake_timeout_input")
                        )
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = autoCashTimeoutText,
                            onValueChange = { autoCashTimeoutText = it },
                            label = { Text("AutoCash", fontSize = 10.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_autocash_timeout_input")
                        )
                    }
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = totalWindowTimeoutText,
                            onValueChange = { totalWindowTimeoutText = it },
                            label = { Text("Window Total", fontSize = 10.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_window_total_timeout_input")
                        )
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = minRemWindowText,
                            onValueChange = { minRemWindowText = it },
                            label = { Text("Min Remain", fontSize = 10.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_min_remaining_window_input")
                        )
                    }
                }

                // SECTION 2: CASH OUT SETTINGS
                OverlaySettingsSectionCard(
                    title = "CASH OUT SETTINGS",
                    icon = Icons.Default.Security,
                    trailingBadge = {
                        Surface(
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(999.dp),
                            color = if (localConfig.isAutoCashOutEnabled) {
                                ActionStartEmerald.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                            } else {
                                Color(0xFFFEF3C7)
                            }
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = if (localConfig.isAutoCashOutEnabled) "AUTOMATIC" else "MANUAL TAP",
                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                                color = if (localConfig.isAutoCashOutEnabled) ActionStartEmerald else Color(0xFFB45309),
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("hand_settings_cashout_section")
                ) {
                    OverlayToggleRow(
                        label = "Automatic Cash Out (ON = auto at Target, OFF = manual single-tap Cash Out)",
                        checked = localConfig.isAutoCashOutEnabled,
                        testTag = "hand_settings_auto_cashout_switch",
                        onCheckedChange = { localConfig = localConfig.copy(isAutoCashOutEnabled = it) }
                    )

                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = targetMultText,
                            onValueChange = { targetMultText = it },
                            label = { Text("Target Multiplier (x)", fontSize = 11.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Decimal),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_target_multiplier_input")
                        )
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = minFlightTimeText,
                            onValueChange = { minFlightTimeText = it },
                            label = { Text("Min Flight Time (ms)", fontSize = 11.sp) },
                            singleLine = true,
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("hand_settings_min_flight_input")
                        )
                    }
                }

                if (onToggleActionHand != null || onOpenGeneralSettings != null) {
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        if (onToggleActionHand != null) {
                            OutlinedButton(
                                onClick = onToggleActionHand,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .testTag("hand_settings_toggle_hand_btn")
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.TouchApp, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text("Show/Hide Hand", fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                        if (onOpenGeneralSettings != null) {
                            OutlinedButton(
                                onClick = onOpenGeneralSettings,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .testTag("hand_settings_general_btn")
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.Settings, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text("General Settings", fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (!validationError.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Surface(
                        color = Color(0xFFFEE2E2),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .testTag("hand_settings_error_banner")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = validationError ?: "",
                            color = ActionStopRed,
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(8.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            HorizontalDivider()

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                OutlinedButton(
                    onClick = onClose,
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp)
                ) {
                    Text("CANCEL", fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        val stake = baseStakeText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
                        val targetMult = targetMultText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
                        val gameAutoTarget = gameAutoTargetText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
                        val minFlight = minFlightTimeText.trim().toLongOrNull()
                        val stakeTimeout = stakeTimeoutText.trim().toLongOrNull()
                        val autoCashTimeout = autoCashTimeoutText.trim().toLongOrNull()
                        val totalTimeout = totalWindowTimeoutText.trim().toLongOrNull()
                        val minRem = minRemWindowText.trim().toLongOrNull()

                        val invalid = stake == null || !stake.isFinite() || stake < 1.0 ||
                            targetMult == null || !targetMult.isFinite() || targetMult < 1.01 ||
                            (localConfig.gameAutoCashOutEnabled && (gameAutoTarget == null || !gameAutoTarget.isFinite() || gameAutoTarget < 1.01)) ||
                            minFlight == null || minFlight !in 0L..60_000L ||
                            stakeTimeout == null || stakeTimeout !in 150L..5_000L ||
                            autoCashTimeout == null || autoCashTimeout !in 150L..5_000L ||
                            totalTimeout == null || totalTimeout !in 500L..15_000L ||
                            minRem == null || minRem !in 300L..10_000L

                        if (invalid) {
                            validationError = "Invalid input: Stake >= 1.0, Targets >= 1.01, Flight 0–60000ms, Timeouts 150–15000ms."
                            runCatching { Toast.makeText(context, validationError, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
                            return@Button
                        }

                        validationError = null
                        onSaveHandSettings(
                            localConfig.copy(
                                baseStake = stake!!,
                                targetMultiplier = targetMult!!,
                                gameAutoCashOutTarget = gameAutoTarget ?: localConfig.gameAutoCashOutTarget,
                                minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs = minFlight!!,
                                stakeEditTimeoutMs = stakeTimeout!!,
                                autoCashOutTimeoutMs = autoCashTimeout!!,
                                betWindowTotalTimeoutMs = totalTimeout!!,
                                minRemainingBetWindowMs = minRem!!
                            )
                        )
                    },
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .heightIn(min = 48.dp)
                        .testTag("hand_settings_save_btn")
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Save, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text("SAVE HAND SETTINGS", fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/service/ScreenColorSampler.kt
====================================================================================================
package com.example.service

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Rect
import com.example.data.model.ColorClassifier
import com.example.data.model.CrashColorCategory
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference

data class PixelColorSample(
    val hex: String,
    val category: CrashColorCategory,
    val isPixelVerified: Boolean
)

/**
 * Thread-safe screen color sampler backed by optional user-consented MediaProjection frames.
 * Reads actual foreground badge/pill pixels inside node bounding rectangles without ever blocking UI.
 */
object ScreenColorSampler {
    private val latestFrameRef = AtomicReference<Bitmap?>(null)

    private val _isCaptureActive = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isCaptureActive: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isCaptureActive.asStateFlow()

    private val _lastCaptureError = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val lastCaptureError: StateFlow<String?> = _lastCaptureError.asStateFlow()

    fun setCaptureActive(active: Boolean, error: String? = null) {
        _isCaptureActive.value = active
        _lastCaptureError.value = error
        if (!active) {
            updateFrame(null)
            ScreenOcrAnalyzer.release()
        }
    }

    fun updateFrame(bitmap: Bitmap?) {
        val old = latestFrameRef.getAndSet(bitmap)
        if (old != null && old !== bitmap && !old.isRecycled) {
            runCatching { old.recycle() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Best-effort read of an on/off switch from pixels: an ON switch is filled with a saturated colour (green),
     * an OFF switch is dark grey with a grey knob. Returns null when no frame is available or the area is too small,
     * so callers never act on a guess.
     */
    fun isSwitchOn(bounds: Rect?): Boolean? {
        if (bounds == null || bounds.isEmpty) return null
        val bmp = latestFrameRef.get() ?: return null
        if (bmp.isRecycled || bmp.width <= 0 || bmp.height <= 0) return null
        val left = bounds.left.coerceIn(0, bmp.width - 1)
        val right = bounds.right.coerceIn(left + 1, bmp.width)
        val top = bounds.top.coerceIn(0, bmp.height - 1)
        val bottom = bounds.bottom.coerceIn(top + 1, bmp.height)
        val w = right - left
        val h = bottom - top
        if (w < 6 || h < 4) return null
        val stepX = (w / 10).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val stepY = (h / 5).coerceAtLeast(1)
        var total = 0
        var vivid = 0
        var y = top + stepY / 2
        while (y < bottom) {
            var x = left + stepX / 2
            while (x < right) {
                val pixel = try { bmp.getPixel(x, y) } catch (_: Throwable) { x += stepX; continue }
                val r = ((pixel ushr 16) and 0xFF) / 255f
                val g = ((pixel ushr 8) and 0xFF) / 255f
                val b = (pixel and 0xFF) / 255f
                val maxC = maxOf(r, g, b)
                val minC = minOf(r, g, b)
                val sat = if (maxC <= 0f) 0f else (maxC - minC) / maxC
                total++
                if (sat > 0.45f && maxC > 0.35f) vivid++
                x += stepX
            }
            y += stepY
        }
        if (total < 6) return null
        return vivid.toFloat() / total.toFloat() > 0.20f
    }

    /**
     * Samples the dominant vivid non-background color inside [bounds] on the latest captured frame.
     * Falls back to interior center pixels if dark/light grayscale frame dominates.
     */
    fun sampleColorInBounds(bounds: Rect?): PixelColorSample? {
        if (bounds == null || bounds.isEmpty) return null
        val bmp = latestFrameRef.get() ?: return null
        if (bmp.isRecycled || bmp.width <= 0 || bmp.height <= 0) return null

        val left = bounds.left.coerceIn(0, bmp.width - 1)
        val right = bounds.right.coerceIn(left + 1, bmp.width)
        val top = bounds.top.coerceIn(0, bmp.height - 1)
        val bottom = bounds.bottom.coerceIn(top + 1, bmp.height)
        val w = right - left
        val h = bottom - top
        if (w < 2 || h < 2) return null

        val stepX = (w / 6).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val stepY = (h / 4).coerceAtLeast(1)

        var bestVividArgb: Int? = null
        var bestVividScore = -1f
        val categoryCounts = mutableMapOf<CrashColorCategory, Int>()
        val representativeByCat = mutableMapOf<CrashColorCategory, Int>()

        var y = top + (h / 6)
        while (y < bottom - (h / 6).coerceAtLeast(1)) {
            var x = left + (w / 8)
            while (x < right - (w / 8).coerceAtLeast(1)) {
                val pixel = try {
                    bmp.getPixel(x, y)
                } catch (_: Throwable) {
                    x += stepX
                    continue
                }
                val r = ((pixel ushr 16) and 0xFF) / 255f
                val g = ((pixel ushr 8) and 0xFF) / 255f
                val b = (pixel and 0xFF) / 255f
                val maxC = maxOf(r, g, b)
                val minC = minOf(r, g, b)
                val sat = if (maxC <= 0f) 0f else (maxC - minC) / maxC

                val cat = ColorClassifier.classifyArgb(pixel)
                if (cat != CrashColorCategory.Other) {
                    categoryCounts[cat] = (categoryCounts[cat] ?: 0) + 1
                    representativeByCat.putIfAbsent(cat, pixel)
                    val score = sat * maxC
                    if (score > bestVividScore) {
                        bestVividScore = score
                        bestVividArgb = pixel
                    }
                }
                x += stepX
            }
            y += stepY
        }

        val dominantCategory = categoryCounts.maxByOrNull { it.value }?.key
        val chosenArgb = representativeByCat[dominantCategory] ?: bestVividArgb ?: return null
        val finalCat = ColorClassifier.classifyArgb(chosenArgb)
        return PixelColorSample(
            hex = ColorClassifier.formatArgbHex(chosenArgb),
            category = finalCat,
            isPixelVerified = true
        )
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/service/ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService.kt
====================================================================================================
package com.example.service

import android.util.Log
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent
import com.example.data.engine.GameAdapterRegistry
import com.example.data.engine.GameDetection
import com.example.data.model.MonitoringStatus
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

data class GameDetectionState(
    val isGameDetected: Boolean = false,
    val platformName: String = "Unknown",
    val displayedGameName: String = "",
    val gameId: String = "",
    val roundId: String? = null,
    val detectedRoundId: String? = null,
    val originalDetectedTitle: String = "",
    val supportedPlatforms: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val isReady: Boolean = false,
    val gamePhase: String = "WAITING_FOR_GAME",
    val missingElements: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val detectedMultiplier: Double? = null,
    val foregroundPackage: String = "",
    val liveColorHex: String? = null,
    val isLiveColorPixelVerified: Boolean = false,
    val liveDetectionLost: Boolean = false,
    val detectionConfidence: Float = 1f,
    val windowAccessStatus: MonitoringStatus = MonitoringStatus.MONITORING,
    val gameWindowAccessible: Boolean = true,
    val gameWindowSuspended: Boolean = false,
    val uiTargets: DetectedUiTargets = DetectedUiTargets(),
    val updatedAtMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

class ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService : AccessibilityController() {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "PredictManagerA11ySvc"
        @Volatile
        var instance: ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService? = null
            private set

        private val _connectionStatus = MutableStateFlow(AccessibilityConnectionStatus.DISCONNECTED)
        val connectionStatus: StateFlow<AccessibilityConnectionStatus> = _connectionStatus.asStateFlow()

        fun isServiceConnected(): Boolean =
            _connectionStatus.value == AccessibilityConnectionStatus.CONNECTED && instance != null

        fun markConnecting() {
            if (_connectionStatus.value != AccessibilityConnectionStatus.CONNECTED) {
                _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.CONNECTING
            }
        }

        private val _detectionState = MutableStateFlow(GameDetectionState())
        val detectionState: StateFlow<GameDetectionState> = _detectionState.asStateFlow()

        fun buildAndPublishFusedState(
            rawTargets: DetectedUiTargets,
            packageName: String,
            ocrSnapshot: OcrFrameSnapshot? = ScreenOcrAnalyzer.latestOcrSnapshot.value,
            nowMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
        ): GameDetectionState {
            val ocrHints = ocrSnapshot?.elements?.map { it.text }.orEmpty()
            val combinedRawTitles = (rawTargets.visibleRawTitles + ocrHints).distinct()
            val hasMultiplierSignals = rawTargets.detectedMultiplier != null ||
                rawTargets.ribbonMultipliers.isNotEmpty() ||
                rawTargets.isBetBtnDetected ||
                rawTargets.isCashOutBtnDetected ||
                rawTargets.isCancelBtnDetected ||
                ocrHints.isNotEmpty()

            val resolvedAdapter = GameAdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(
                packageName = packageName,
                url = rawTargets.detectedUrl,
                pageHints = rawTargets.pageTextHints,
                rawTexts = combinedRawTitles,
                hasMultiplierSignals = hasMultiplierSignals
            )

            val fusedResult = ScreenOcrAnalyzer.fuseAccessibilityAndOcr(
                a11yTargets = rawTargets.copy(
                    foregroundPackage = packageName.ifBlank { rawTargets.foregroundPackage }
                ),
                ocrSnapshot = ocrSnapshot,
                adapter = resolvedAdapter,
                nowMs = nowMs
            )
            val targets = fusedResult.fusedTargets

            val platformName = GameAdapterRegistry.resolvePlatformDisplay(
                packageName = packageName,
                url = targets.detectedUrl,
                pageHints = targets.pageTextHints
            )

            val resolvedGameTitle = resolvedAdapter?.displayedGameName
                ?: fusedResult.ocrParsed?.detectedGameName
                ?: GameDetection.resolvePlatformFromSignals(
                    packageName = packageName,
                    detectedUrl = targets.detectedUrl,
                    pageHints = targets.pageTextHints
                )
                ?: platformName.takeIf { it != "Unknown" }
                ?: "Detected Game"

            val resolvedGameId = resolvedAdapter?.gameId?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && it != "unknown" }
                ?: fusedResult.ocrParsed?.detectedGameId?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                ?: resolvedGameTitle.lowercase().replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9]+"), "_").trim('_').ifBlank { "unknown" }

            var confidence = 0f
            if (resolvedAdapter != null && resolvedAdapter.gameId != "unknown") confidence += 0.45f
            else if (resolvedAdapter != null) confidence += 0.25f
            if (!targets.detectedUrl.isNullOrBlank() || platformName != "Unknown") confidence += 0.20f
            if (targets.detectedMultiplier != null) confidence += 0.15f
            if (targets.ribbonMultipliers.isNotEmpty()) confidence += 0.10f
            if (targets.isBetBtnDetected || targets.isCashOutBtnDetected || targets.isCancelBtnDetected || targets.isStakeInputDetected) {
                confidence += 0.15f
            }
            if (fusedResult.hasConflict || fusedResult.isOcrUncertain) {
                confidence = (confidence * 0.5f).coerceAtMost(0.45f)
            }
            val boundedConfidence = confidence.coerceIn(0f, 1f)

            // A browser package, a visible balance or arbitrary OCR text is NOT a crash game. Require a recognised
            // game adapter, or real crash-game controls/numbers on screen (live multiplier, history ribbon,
            // Bet / Cancel / Cash Out control).
            val hasCrashGameSignals = targets.detectedMultiplier != null ||
                targets.ribbonMultipliers.size >= 3 ||
                targets.isBetBtnDetected ||
                targets.isCashOutBtnDetected ||
                targets.isCancelBtnDetected
            val gameDetected = (resolvedAdapter != null && resolvedAdapter.gameId != "unknown") || hasCrashGameSignals

            val missing = buildList {
                if (!targets.isBetBtnDetected && !targets.isCancelBtnDetected &&
                    !targets.isCashOutBtnDetected
                ) add("Bet Control Target")
                if (!targets.isCashOutBtnDetected) add("Cash Out Target")
                if (!targets.isStakeInputDetected && !targets.isDoubleBtnDetected &&
                    !targets.isAddBtnDetected
                ) add("Stake Input Control")
                if (fusedResult.hasConflict) add("A11y/OCR Conflict")
                if (fusedResult.isOcrUncertain) add("OCR Uncertain")
            }

            val allSupportedPlatforms = (resolvedAdapter?.supportedPlatforms
                ?: GameAdapterRegistry.allAdapters().flatMap { it.supportedPlatforms }).distinct()

            val nextState = GameDetectionState(
                isGameDetected = gameDetected,
                platformName = platformName,
                displayedGameName = resolvedGameTitle,
                gameId = resolvedGameId,
                roundId = targets.detectedCurrentRoundId,
                detectedRoundId = targets.detectedCurrentRoundId,
                originalDetectedTitle = resolvedGameTitle,
                supportedPlatforms = allSupportedPlatforms,
                isReady = gameDetected && hasMultiplierSignals && !fusedResult.hasConflict,
                gamePhase = targets.gamePhase,
                missingElements = missing,
                detectedMultiplier = targets.detectedMultiplier,
                foregroundPackage = packageName,
                detectionConfidence = boundedConfidence,
                gameWindowAccessible = true,
                uiTargets = targets,
                updatedAtMs = nowMs
            )
            _detectionState.value = nextState
            return nextState
        }

        fun recomputeWithLatestOcr(
            ocrSnapshot: OcrFrameSnapshot,
            nowMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
        ): GameDetectionState? {
            if (!isServiceConnected()) return null
            val current = _detectionState.value
            val pkg = current.foregroundPackage.ifBlank { current.uiTargets.foregroundPackage }
            if (pkg.isBlank() && !current.isGameDetected) return null
            val rawTargets = instance?.getLatestUiTargets() ?: current.uiTargets
            return buildAndPublishFusedState(
                rawTargets = rawTargets,
                packageName = pkg,
                ocrSnapshot = ocrSnapshot,
                nowMs = nowMs
            )
        }
    }

    /** Shared action executor. StrategyEngine remains the authority for strategy decisions. */
    val actionExecutor: ScreenEarnerActionExecutor by lazy(LazyThreadSafetyMode.NONE) {
        ScreenEarnerActionExecutor(this)
    }

    /** Optional ordered UI macro runner; it does not collect multipliers or create strategies. */
    val macroRunner: ScreenEarnerMacroRunner by lazy(LazyThreadSafetyMode.NONE) {
        ScreenEarnerMacroRunner(actionExecutor)
    }

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        try {
            super.onServiceConnected()
            instance = this
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.CONNECTED
            Log.i(TAG, "ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService connected successfully.")
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.ERROR
            Log.e(TAG, "Error in ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService.onServiceConnected", t)
        }
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        try {
            if (event == null) return

            val packageName = runCatching { event.packageName?.toString().orEmpty() }
                .getOrDefault("")
            val ownPkg = runCatching { applicationContext?.packageName }.getOrNull()

            // Ignore Predict Manager's own overlay events; do not let its controls become game targets.
            if (!ownPkg.isNullOrBlank() && packageName == ownPkg) return

            super.onAccessibilityEvent(event)

            buildAndPublishFusedState(
                rawTargets = detectedTargets.value,
                packageName = packageName,
                ocrSnapshot = ScreenOcrAnalyzer.latestOcrSnapshot.value,
                nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error processing accessibility event", t)
        }
    }

    fun performClickAtCoordinates(
        x: Float,
        y: Float,
        onComplete: ((Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
    ) {
        dispatchTouchGesture(x, y, onComplete)
    }

    override fun onInterrupt() {
        try {
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.ERROR
            macroRunner.stop()
            super.onInterrupt()
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error in onInterrupt", t)
        }
    }

    override fun onUnbind(intent: android.content.Intent?): Boolean {
        return try {
            macroRunner.stop()
            if (instance === this) instance = null
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.DISCONNECTED
            super.onUnbind(intent)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.ERROR
            Log.e(TAG, "Error in onUnbind", t)
            false
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        try {
            macroRunner.stop()
            super.onDestroy()
            if (instance === this) instance = null
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.DISCONNECTED
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            _connectionStatus.value = AccessibilityConnectionStatus.ERROR
            Log.e(TAG, "Error in onDestroy", t)
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================

    val multiWindowBundle: StateFlow<MultiWindowAnalysisBundle> = AutomationController.multiWindowBundle

    private val _selectedTimeRangePreset = MutableStateFlow(HourlyAnalysisEngine.TimeRangePreset.ONE_HOUR)
    val selectedTimeRangePreset: StateFlow<HourlyAnalysisEngine.TimeRangePreset> = _selectedTimeRangePreset.asStateFlow()

    private val _customRangeStartMs = MutableStateFlow(System.currentTimeMillis() - 6 * 3_600_000L)
    val customRangeStartMs: StateFlow<Long> = _customRangeStartMs.asStateFlow()

    private val _customRangeEndMs = MutableStateFlow(System.currentTimeMillis())
    val customRangeEndMs: StateFlow<Long> = _customRangeEndMs.asStateFlow()

    val timeRangeMetrics: StateFlow<HourlyWindowMetrics> = combine(
        crashRecords,
        selectedGameId,
        strategyConfig,
        selectedTimeRangePreset,
        combine(_customRangeStartMs, _customRangeEndMs) { s, e -> s to e }
    ) { records, gameId, cfg, preset, customPair ->
        val resolvedName = when {
            gameId == "ALL" -> "All Games"
            gameId == "unknown" -> "Unknown"
            else -> GameAdapterRegistry.allAdapters().firstOrNull { it.gameId == gameId }?.displayedGameName
                ?: records.lastOrNull { it.gameId == gameId }?.gameName
                ?: "Unknown"
        }
        HourlyAnalysisEngine.analyzeTimeRange(
            preset = preset,
            records = records,
            config = cfg ?: StrategyConfig(),
            gameId = gameId,
            gameName = resolvedName,
            customStartMs = customPair.first,
            customEndMs = customPair.second
        )
    }.stateIn(
        scope = viewModelScope,
        started = SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        initialValue = HourlyAnalysisEngine.analyzeTimeRange(
            preset = HourlyAnalysisEngine.TimeRangePreset.ONE_HOUR,
            records = emptyList()
        )
    )

    private val _selectedTab = MutableStateFlow(MainActivity.TAB_HOME)
    val selectedTab: StateFlow<Int> = _selectedTab.asStateFlow()

    private val _activeRoute = MutableStateFlow(AppRoute.HOME)
    val activeRoute: StateFlow<AppRoute> = _activeRoute.asStateFlow()

    private val _previousTab = MutableStateFlow(MainActivity.TAB_HOME)
    val previousTab: StateFlow<Int> = _previousTab.asStateFlow()

    private val _isOverlayServiceRunning = MutableStateFlow(ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning)
    val isOverlayServiceRunning: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isOverlayServiceRunning.asStateFlow()

    private val _userMessage = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val userMessage: StateFlow<String?> = _userMessage.asStateFlow()

    fun refreshOverlayRunningState() {
        _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning
    }

    fun setSelectedGameId(gameId: String) {
        AutomationController.setSelectedGameId(gameId)
    }

    fun navigateToRoute(route: AppRoute) {
        selectTab(route.tabIndex)
    }

    fun selectTab(index: Int) {
        val current = _selectedTab.value
        val coerced = index.coerceIn(MainActivity.TAB_HOME, MainActivity.TAB_RISK_MANAGER)
        if (coerced != current) {
            if (current == MainActivity.TAB_HOME ||
                current == MainActivity.TAB_LIVE_MONITOR ||
                current == MainActivity.TAB_CRASH_HISTORY ||
                current == MainActivity.TAB_PATTERNS ||
                current == MainActivity.TAB_HOURLY_ANALYSIS ||
                current == MainActivity.TAB_NOTIFICATIONS
            ) {
                _previousTab.value = current
            }
            _selectedTab.value = coerced
            _activeRoute.value = AppRoute.fromTabIndex(coerced)
        }
    }

    fun closeEditorScreen() {
        val current = _selectedTab.value
        val candidatePrev = _previousTab.value
        val target = if (candidatePrev != current &&
            candidatePrev != MainActivity.TAB_STRATEGY &&
            candidatePrev != MainActivity.TAB_SETTINGS &&
            candidatePrev != MainActivity.TAB_RISK_MANAGER
        ) {
            candidatePrev
        } else {
            MainActivity.TAB_HOME
        }
        _selectedTab.value = target
        _activeRoute.value = AppRoute.fromTabIndex(target)
    }

    fun openInOverlayMode(context: Context, route: AppRoute = _activeRoute.value) {
        try {
            if (!Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)) {
                toggleOverlayService(context)
                return
            }
            val intent = Intent(context, ScreenEarnerOverlayService::class.java).apply {
                action = ScreenEarnerOverlayService.ACTION_OPEN_OVERLAY_ROUTE
                putExtra(ScreenEarnerOverlayService.EXTRA_OVERLAY_ROUTE, route.name)
            }
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(intent)
            } else {
                context.startService(intent)
            }
            _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = true
            _userMessage.value = "Switched to Floating Overlay (${route.shortLabel})"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed switching to overlay mode", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Unable to open overlay container"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Starts observation-only Live Game Monitoring (does NOT require calibrated Bet hand).
     */
    fun startAutomation() {
        startMonitoring()
    }

    fun startMonitoring() {
        try {
            val result = AutomationController.startMonitoringSession(
                isAccessibilityConnected = ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService.isServiceConnected()
            )
            _userMessage.value = if (result.started) {
                "Predict Manager Live Monitor STARTED"
            } else {
                result.message
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to start monitoring", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Failed to start monitoring"
        }
    }

    fun pauseAutomation() {
        try {
            AutomationController.pauseMonitoringSession()
            _userMessage.value = "Monitoring PAUSED"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to pause monitoring", e)
        }
    }

    fun resumeAutomation() {
        try {
            val result = AutomationController.startMonitoringSession(
                isAccessibilityConnected = ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService.isServiceConnected()
            )
            _userMessage.value = if (result.started) "Monitoring RESUMED" else result.message
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to resume monitoring", e)
        }
    }

    fun quitAutomation() {
        try {
            AutomationController.stopMonitoringSession()
            _userMessage.value = "Monitoring STOPPED"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to stop monitoring", e)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Independent Automation Assistant controls (never stops or interrupts live Monitoring Engine).
     */
    fun startAutomationAssistant() {
        try {
            val result = AutomationController.startAutomationAssistant(
                isAccessibilityConnected = ScreenEarnerAccessibilityService.isServiceConnected()
            )
            _userMessage.value = if (result.started) "Automation Assistant ACTIVE" else result.message
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed starting automation assistant", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Unable to start Automation Assistant"
        }
    }

    fun pauseAutomationAssistant() {
        try {
            AutomationController.pauseAutomationAssistant()
            _userMessage.value = "Automation Assistant PAUSED (Monitoring continues)"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed pausing automation assistant", e)
        }
    }

    fun stopAutomationAssistant() {
        try {
            AutomationController.stopAutomationAssistant()
            _userMessage.value = "Automation Assistant STOPPED (Monitoring continues)"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed stopping automation assistant", e)
        }
    }

    fun endSessionAndClearSessionData() {
        try {
            AutomationController.requestEndSessionAndClearSessionData()
            _userMessage.value = "Session ended & current session data cleared"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed clearing current session data", e)
        }
    }

    fun exitUtility(context: Context) {
        try {
            AutomationController.stopMonitoringSession()
            BatteryOptimizationWorker.cancel(context)
            val nm = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as? android.app.NotificationManager
            nm?.cancelAll()
            context.stopService(Intent(context, ScreenEarnerOverlayService::class.java))
            context.stopService(Intent(context, FloatingOverlayService::class.java))
            context.stopService(Intent(context, ScreenCaptureProjectionService::class.java))
            _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = false
            _userMessage.value = "Predict Manager floating utility stopped."
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed exiting overlay utility", e)
        }
    }

    private fun sanitizeStrategyConfig(config: StrategyConfig): StrategyConfig {
        val sanitizedRules = config.getRuleConfigs().map { rule ->
            rule.copy(
                customAddAmount = rule.customAddAmount.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 10.0,
                targetWinsToFetch = rule.targetWinsToFetch.coerceAtLeast(1),
                requiredLosses = rule.requiredLosses.coerceAtLeast(0),
                waitMinutes = rule.waitMinutes.coerceAtLeast(0),
                minMultiplier = rule.minMultiplier.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 0.0,
                maxMultiplier = rule.maxMultiplier.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 1000.0
            )
        }
        val safeLow = config.lowThreshold.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: 2.00
        val safeBoundary = config.boundaryValue.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: safeLow
        val safeBig = config.bigThreshold.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it > safeLow } ?: 10.00
        return config.copy(
            lowThreshold = safeLow,
            boundaryValue = safeBoundary,
            bigThreshold = safeBig,
            gridRows = config.gridRows.coerceIn(4, 5),
            gridColumns = config.gridColumns.coerceIn(7, 8),
            strategy1LowThreshold = config.strategy1LowThreshold.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: 2.00,
            strategy1RequiredLowRounds = config.strategy1RequiredLowRounds.coerceAtLeast(1),
            strategy2HighThreshold = config.strategy2HighThreshold.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: 10.00,
            strategy2WaitRounds = config.strategy2WaitRounds.coerceAtLeast(1),
            baseStake = config.baseStake.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it > 0.0 } ?: 10.0,
            multiplierProgression = config.multiplierProgression.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.0 } ?: 2.0,
            stopAfterConsecutiveWins = config.stopAfterConsecutiveWins.coerceAtLeast(1),
            targetMultiplier = config.targetMultiplier.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: 2.00,
            sessionTimeoutMinutes = config.sessionTimeoutMinutes.coerceAtLeast(1),
            scheduleStartHour = config.scheduleStartHour.coerceIn(0, 23),
            scheduleStartMinute = config.scheduleStartMinute.coerceIn(0, 59),
            scheduleEndHour = config.scheduleEndHour.coerceIn(0, 23),
            scheduleEndMinute = config.scheduleEndMinute.coerceIn(0, 59),
            minDetectionConfidence = config.minDetectionConfidence.takeIf { it.isFinite() }?.coerceIn(0f, 1f) ?: 0.5f,
            preferredGameId = config.preferredGameId.ifBlank { "ALL" },
            minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs = config.minFlightTimeBeforeCashOutMs.coerceIn(0L, 60_000L),
            maxBetRetries = config.maxBetRetries.coerceIn(0, 10),
            retryDelayMs = config.retryDelayMs.coerceIn(100L, 5_000L),
            bettingWindowExpiryMs = config.bettingWindowExpiryMs.coerceIn(1_000L, 120_000L),
            lossProgressionAddAmount = config.lossProgressionAddAmount.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 10.0,
            gameAutoCashOutTarget = config.gameAutoCashOutTarget.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 1.01 } ?: 2.00,
            stakeEditTimeoutMs = config.stakeEditTimeoutMs.coerceIn(150L, 5_000L),
            autoCashOutTimeoutMs = config.autoCashOutTimeoutMs.coerceIn(150L, 5_000L),
            betWindowTotalTimeoutMs = config.betWindowTotalTimeoutMs.coerceIn(500L, 15_000L),
            minRemainingBetWindowMs = config.minRemainingBetWindowMs.coerceIn(300L, 10_000L),
            minStake = config.minStake.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 0.0,
            maxStake = config.maxStake.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 0.0
        ).withUpdatedRuleConfigs(sanitizedRules)
    }

    private fun sanitizeRiskLimits(limits: RiskLimits): RiskLimits {
        return limits.copy(
            maxSingleStake = limits.maxSingleStake.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it > 0.0 } ?: 500.0,
            maxSessionLoss = limits.maxSessionLoss.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 1000.0,
            maxTotalRounds = limits.maxTotalRounds.coerceAtLeast(1),
            maxProgressionLevel = limits.maxProgressionLevel.coerceAtLeast(1),
            profitTarget = limits.profitTarget.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 1500.0,
            minBalanceThreshold = limits.minBalanceThreshold.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 50.0,
            maxSequenceExposure = limits.maxSequenceExposure.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 500.0,
            maxDailyLoss = limits.maxDailyLoss.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 500.0,
            safetyReserveAmount = limits.safetyReserveAmount.takeIf { it.isFinite() && it >= 0.0 } ?: 0.0
        )
    }

    fun applyStrategySelection(config: StrategyConfig) {
        try {
            val safeConfig = sanitizeStrategyConfig(config)
            strategyEngine.bindStrategyConfig(safeConfig)
            viewModelScope.launch {
                try {
                    repository.saveStrategyConfig(safeConfig)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Save failed while applying config", e)
                    _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Save failed while binding config", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
        }
    }

    fun saveStrategyConfig(config: StrategyConfig, ensureOverlayActive: Boolean = false, context: Context? = null) {
        try {
            val safeConfig = sanitizeStrategyConfig(config)
            strategyEngine.bindStrategyConfig(safeConfig)
            if (ensureOverlayActive && context != null) {
                ensureOverlayServiceRunning(context)
            }
            viewModelScope.launch {
                try {
                    repository.saveStrategyConfig(safeConfig)
                    _userMessage.value = "Settings Saved"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Save failed for config", e)
                    _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Save failed for config", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
        }
    }

    fun saveRiskLimits(limits: RiskLimits, ensureOverlayActive: Boolean = false, context: Context? = null) {
        try {
            val safeLimits = sanitizeRiskLimits(limits)
            if (ensureOverlayActive && context != null) {
                ensureOverlayServiceRunning(context)
            }
            viewModelScope.launch {
                try {
                    repository.saveRiskLimits(safeLimits)
                    _userMessage.value = "Safety Locks Saved"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Save failed for limits", e)
                    _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Save failed for limits", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
        }
    }

    fun updateGridDimensions(rows: Int, cols: Int) {
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        applyStrategySelection(current.copy(gridRows = rows.coerceIn(4, 5), gridColumns = cols.coerceIn(7, 8)))
    }

    fun updateBaseStake(newBaseStake: Double) {
        if (!newBaseStake.isFinite() || newBaseStake < 1.0) {
            _userMessage.value = "Base stake must be at least 1.0"
            return
        }
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(current.copy(baseStake = newBaseStake))
    }

    fun updateTargetMultiplier(newMultiplier: Double) {
        if (!newMultiplier.isFinite() || newMultiplier < 1.01) {
            _userMessage.value = "Target multiplier must be at least 1.01x"
            return
        }
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(current.copy(targetMultiplier = newMultiplier))
    }

    fun toggleAutoBet(enabled: Boolean) {
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(current.copy(isAutoBetEnabled = enabled))
        _userMessage.value = if (enabled) "Automatic Bet enabled" else "Manual Bet mode active (Tap Hand to Bet)"
    }

    fun toggleAutoCashOut(enabled: Boolean) {
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(current.copy(isAutoCashOutEnabled = enabled))
        _userMessage.value = if (enabled) "Automatic Cash Out enabled" else "Manual Cash Out mode active (Tap Hand to Cash Out)"
    }

    fun updateLossProgression(enabled: Boolean, mode: StakeProgressionMode, addAmount: Double) {
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(
            current.copy(
                lossProgressionEnabled = enabled,
                lossProgressionMode = mode,
                lossProgressionAddAmount = addAmount
            )
        )
    }

    fun updateCategoryThresholds(low: Double, boundary: Double, big: Double) {
        if (!low.isFinite() || !boundary.isFinite() || !big.isFinite() || low < 1.01 || big <= low) {
            _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
            return
        }
        val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
        saveStrategyConfig(current.copy(lowThreshold = low, boundaryValue = boundary, bigThreshold = big))
    }

    fun applyManualCrashCorrection(
        recordId: Long,
        newMultiplier: Double,
        newCategory: CrashColorCategory,
        reason: String
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (!newMultiplier.isFinite() || newMultiplier < 1.0) {
                    _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
                    return@launch
                }
                val ok = repository.applyManualCrashCorrection(
                    recordId = recordId,
                    newMultiplier = newMultiplier,
                    newColorHex = newCategory.defaultHex,
                    newCategory = newCategory,
                    reason = reason.ifBlank { "Manual operator verification" }
                )
                _userMessage.value = if (ok) {
                    "Crash record updated with audit trail."
                } else {
                    "Crash record not found."
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Manual crash correction failed", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to save correction"
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveCustomStrategy(strategy: CustomPatternStrategy) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (strategy.name.isBlank()) {
                    _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
                    return@launch
                }
                if (strategy.id == 0L) {
                    repository.insertCustomStrategy(strategy)
                    _userMessage.value = "Strategy '${strategy.name}' created"
                } else {
                    repository.updateCustomStrategy(strategy)
                    _userMessage.value = "Strategy '${strategy.name}' updated"
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Save failed for custom strategy", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
            }
        }
    }

    fun toggleCustomStrategyEnabled(strategy: CustomPatternStrategy, enabled: Boolean) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.updateCustomStrategy(
                    strategy.copy(
                        isEnabled = enabled,
                        currentlyApproaching = if (!enabled) false else strategy.currentlyApproaching,
                        currentlyReached = if (!enabled) false else strategy.currentlyReached
                    )
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed toggling strategy", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun toggleCustomStrategyNotifications(strategy: CustomPatternStrategy, enabled: Boolean) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.updateCustomStrategy(strategy.copy(notificationEnabled = enabled))
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed toggling strategy notifications", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun deleteCustomStrategy(id: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.deleteCustomStrategy(id)
                _userMessage.value = "Strategy deleted"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed deleting strategy", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun swapStrategyOrder(first: CustomPatternStrategy, second: CustomPatternStrategy) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.swapStrategyOrder(first, second)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed reordering strategies", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveAlarmProfile(profile: AlarmProfileEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.upsertAlarmProfile(profile)
                _userMessage.value = "${profile.category().displayTitle} updated"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed saving alarm profile", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
            }
        }
    }

    fun setTimeRangePreset(
        preset: HourlyAnalysisEngine.TimeRangePreset,
        customStartMs: Long = _customRangeStartMs.value,
        customEndMs: Long = _customRangeEndMs.value
    ) {
        _selectedTimeRangePreset.value = preset
        if (preset == HourlyAnalysisEngine.TimeRangePreset.CUSTOM) {
            _customRangeStartMs.value = customStartMs
            _customRangeEndMs.value = customEndMs
        }
    }

    suspend fun countClearScopeRecords(scope: ClearHistoryScope, gameId: String): Int {
        return repository.countClearScopeRecords(
            scope = scope,
            activeSessionId = AutomationController.activeSessionId.value,
            selectedGameId = gameId
        )
    }

    fun clearHistoryByScope(scope: ClearHistoryScope, gameId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                AutomationController.clearHistoryByScope(scope, gameId)
                _userMessage.value = when (scope) {
                    ClearHistoryScope.CURRENT_SESSION -> "Current session crash records cleared"
                    ClearHistoryScope.SELECTED_GAME_ARCHIVED -> "Archived history for selected game cleared"
                    ClearHistoryScope.ALL_HISTORY -> "All local crash history & stats cleared"
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed clearing history by scope $scope", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to clear history"
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveNotificationSettings(settings: NotificationSettings) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.saveNotificationSettings(
                    settings.copy(
                        repeatIntervalMinutes = settings.repeatIntervalMinutes.coerceAtLeast(0),
                        quietStartHour = settings.quietStartHour.coerceIn(0, 23),
                        quietStartMinute = settings.quietStartMinute.coerceIn(0, 59),
                        quietEndHour = settings.quietEndHour.coerceIn(0, 23),
                        quietEndMinute = settings.quietEndMinute.coerceIn(0, 59)
                    )
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed saving notification settings", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
            }
        }
    }

    fun markNotificationOpened(historyId: Long) {
        if (historyId <= 0L) return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.updateNotificationInteraction(historyId, NotificationInteractionStatus.OPENED_BY_USER)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed marking notification opened", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun clearNotificationHistory() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.clearNotificationHistory()
                AutomationController.notificationManager?.clearSessionCache()
                _userMessage.value = "Notification history cleared"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed clearing notification history", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun triggerRibbonHistoryCollection(context: Context = getApplication()) {
        try {
            AutomationController.triggerManualHistoryCollection(context)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed triggering ribbon history collection", e)
        }
    }

    fun stopPixelColorCapture(context: Context) {
        try {
            context.stopService(Intent(context, ScreenCaptureProjectionService::class.java))
            val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
            applyStrategySelection(current.copy(pixelColorCaptureEnabled = false))
            _userMessage.value = "Pixel color capture stopped."
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed stopping pixel capture", e)
        }
    }

    fun onPixelColorConsentResult(context: Context, resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        if (resultCode != android.app.Activity.RESULT_OK || data == null) {
            _userMessage.value = "Pixel color capture permission declined."
            return
        }
        try {
            val intent = ScreenCaptureProjectionService.createStartIntent(context, resultCode, data)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(intent)
            } else {
                context.startService(intent)
            }
            val current = strategyConfig.value ?: StrategyConfig()
            applyStrategySelection(current.copy(pixelColorCaptureEnabled = true))
            _userMessage.value = "Pixel color capture active."
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed starting pixel capture service", e)
            _userMessage.value = "Unable to start pixel color capture."
        }
    }

    fun ensureOverlayServiceRunning(context: Context) {
        try {
            if (!Settings.canDrawOverlays(context) || ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning) {
                _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning
                return
            }
            val intent = Intent(context, ScreenEarnerOverlayService::class.java)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(intent)
            } else {
                context.startService(intent)
            }
            _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to ensure overlay service running", e)
        }
    }

    fun triggerEmergencyStop() {
        try {
            val limits = (riskLimits.value ?: RiskLimits()).copy(emergencyStop = true)
            saveRiskLimits(limits)
            quitAutomation()
            _userMessage.value = "MONITORING LOCK ENGAGED"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to lock monitoring", e)
        }
    }

    fun clearEmergencyStop() {
        try {
            val limits = (riskLimits.value ?: RiskLimits()).copy(emergencyStop = false)
            saveRiskLimits(limits)
            _userMessage.value = "Monitoring Lock Cleared"
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to clear monitoring lock", e)
        }
    }

    fun toggleOverlayService(context: Context) {
        try {
            if (!Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)) {
                val intent = Intent(
                    Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION,
                    android.net.Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")
                ).apply {
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                }
                context.startActivity(intent)
                _userMessage.value = "Please grant Display Over Other Apps permission."
                _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = false
                return
            }

            val intent = Intent(context, ScreenEarnerOverlayService::class.java)
            if (ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning) {
                context.stopService(intent)
                _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = false
                _userMessage.value = "Floating Monitor Stopped."
            } else {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
                } else {
                    context.startService(intent)
                }
                android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                    _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning
                }, 200L)
                _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = true
                _userMessage.value = "Floating Monitor Started."
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to toggle overlay service", e)
            _isOverlayServiceRunning.value = ScreenEarnerOverlayService.isRunning
            _userMessage.value = "Unable to start overlay service: ${e.localizedMessage ?: "Check permissions"}"
        }
    }

    fun clearUserMessage() {
        _userMessage.value = null
    }

    fun clearSessionLogs() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.clearSessionLogs()
                _userMessage.value = "Logs cleared"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed clearing session logs", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun clearMultiplierRecords() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.clearMultiplierRecords()
                repository.clearAllCrashRecords()
                _userMessage.value = "Crash history cleared"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed clearing crash records", e)
            }
        }
    }

    fun setActiveProfile(profileId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.setActiveProfile(profileId)
                _userMessage.value = "Profile activated: $profileId"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Save failed activating profile", e)
                _userMessage.value = "Failed to save settings, please check input"
            }
        }
    }

    // Legacy compatibility stubs so nothing breaks if older composables call them
    fun insertShortcut(shortcut: FloatingShortcut) {
        viewModelScope.launch { runCatching { repository.insertShortcut(shortcut) } }
    }
    fun updateShortcut(shortcut: FloatingShortcut) {
        viewModelScope.launch { runCatching { repository.updateShortcut(shortcut) } }
    }
    fun deleteShortcut(id: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch { runCatching { repository.deleteShortcut(id) } }
    }
    fun addNewShortcut(label: String, type: ShortcutType, colorHex: String) {}
    fun updateShortcutVisualPosition(shortcutId: Long, visualX: Float, visualY: Float) {}
}

FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/AviatorLayoutDetectionTest.kt
====================================================================================================
package com.example

import android.graphics.Rect
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo
import com.example.data.model.SiteButtonState
import com.example.service.AccessibilityController
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import java.util.IdentityHashMap

/**
 * Layouts taken from real BetPawa Aviator screenshots (1080x2400, Chrome):
 *  - "Bet" / "Auto" TABS sit above the stake box and the big green "Bet 400.00 TZS" button.
 *  - Mid-flight the Bet button stays enabled (it queues the next round).
 *  - The Auto tab reveals "Auto bet" / "Auto Cash Out" + switch + "2.00" value box on one row.
 */
@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class AviatorLayoutDetectionTest {

    private class TestController(
        private val tree: IdentityHashMap<AccessibilityNodeInfo, List<AccessibilityNodeInfo>>
    ) : AccessibilityController() {
        override fun childNodesOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): List<AccessibilityNodeInfo> = tree[node] ?: emptyList()
        fun scan(root: AccessibilityNodeInfo) = scanWindowTargets(root)
    }

    private fun node(
        className: String,
        text: String? = null,
        bounds: Rect,
        clickable: Boolean = false,
        editable: Boolean = false
    ): AccessibilityNodeInfo = AccessibilityNodeInfo().apply {
        packageName = "com.android.chrome"
        this.className = className
        this.text = text
        isEnabled = true
        isClickable = clickable
        isEditable = editable
        isFocusable = clickable || editable
        isVisibleToUser = true
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        setBoundsInScreen(bounds)
    }

    private val betButtonRect = Rect(460, 1200, 1033, 1425)
    private val betTabRect = Rect(259, 1088, 540, 1156)
    private val autoTabRect = Rect(540, 1088, 822, 1156)
    private val stakeRect = Rect(110, 1200, 350, 1270)

    private class Layout(
        val root: AccessibilityNodeInfo,
        val controller: TestController,
        val betButton: AccessibilityNodeInfo
    )

    private fun layout(multiplier: String, withAutoRow: Boolean): Layout {
        val root = node("android.widget.FrameLayout", bounds = Rect(0, 0, 1080, 2400))
        val live = node("android.widget.TextView", multiplier, Rect(290, 700, 800, 870))
        val betTab = node("android.widget.Button", "Bet", betTabRect, clickable = true)
        val autoTab = node("android.widget.Button", "Auto", autoTabRect, clickable = true)
        val stake = node("android.widget.EditText", "400.00", stakeRect, editable = true)
        val betButton = node("android.widget.Button", "Bet\n400.00 TZS", betButtonRect, clickable = true)
        val kids = mutableListOf(live, betTab, autoTab, stake, betButton)
        if (withAutoRow) {
            kids += node("android.widget.TextView", "Auto bet", Rect(50, 1495, 190, 1540))
            kids += node("android.widget.Switch", null, Rect(200, 1485, 300, 1550), clickable = true)
            kids += node("android.widget.TextView", "Auto Cash Out", Rect(446, 1495, 680, 1540))
            kids += node("android.widget.Switch", null, Rect(693, 1485, 793, 1550), clickable = true)
            kids += node("android.widget.EditText", "2.00", Rect(810, 1485, 975, 1550), editable = true)
        }
        val tree = IdentityHashMap<AccessibilityNodeInfo, List<AccessibilityNodeInfo>>().apply { put(root, kids) }
        return Layout(root, TestController(tree), betButton)
    }

    @Test
    fun `picks the green Bet button not the Bet tab`() {
        val l = layout("8.72x", withAutoRow = false)
        val scan = l.controller.scan(l.root)
        assertEquals(betButtonRect, scan.betButtonBounds)
    }

    @Test
    fun `plane flying with Bet button visible is IN_FLIGHT not BETTING_OPEN`() {
        val l = layout("8.72x", withAutoRow = false)
        val scan = l.controller.scan(l.root)
        assertEquals("IN_FLIGHT", scan.gamePhase)
        assertEquals(SiteButtonState.IDLE_READY_TO_BET, scan.siteButtonState)
    }

    @Test
    fun `Bet button without a live multiplier is BETTING_OPEN and can queue a bet`() {
        val l = layout("", withAutoRow = false)
        val scan = l.controller.scan(l.root)
        assertEquals("BETTING_OPEN", scan.gamePhase)
        assertTrue(scan.canQueueBet)
    }

    @Test
    fun `Auto Cash Out switch and value box are matched by row and never reported as stake`() {
        val l = layout("46.27x", withAutoRow = true)
        val scan = l.controller.scan(l.root)
        assertTrue(scan.isAutoCashOutRowVisible)
        assertEquals(Rect(693, 1485, 793, 1550), scan.autoCashOutToggleBounds)
        assertEquals(Rect(810, 1485, 975, 1550), scan.autoCashOutInputBounds)
        assertEquals(2.00, scan.detectedAutoCashOutValue!!, 0.001)
        assertEquals(stakeRect, scan.stakeInputBounds)
    }

    @Test
    fun `Auto tab is reported when the Auto Cash Out row is hidden`() {
        val l = layout("46.27x", withAutoRow = false)
        val scan = l.controller.scan(l.root)
        assertFalse(scan.isAutoCashOutRowVisible)
        assertNotNull(scan.autoCashOutTabBounds)
        assertEquals(autoTabRect, scan.autoCashOutTabBounds)
    }
}
